## [5.270.2] — 2026-09-09 — the 6.0.0 scope was assembled by hand three times and was wrong twice, so it is derived now
- **What changed.** New `engine/major_readiness.js` — it answers *would 6.0.0 be honest today, and what would it have to say*, by REUSING `engine/docs_scan.js` and `engine/quarantine.js` through their exports rather than recounting anything. It puts side by side the three different questions a figure can fail at a major, which is the thing that did not exist: each hand-count tonight was correct about its own question, and the one being quoted was whichever had been run last. Separately, `data/abra-tags.js` was rebuilt from `data/tags.json` after batch S/T moved the tag derivation and left the browser copy behind.
- **Measured.** **The living-document rewrite is 119 figure-level edits, not the 85–90 estimated by hand** — 13 resting on a withheld artifact, 84 stale citations, 22 untraceable, across the five documents in the major fold-in. A re-run today would **LIFT 40 artifacts and leave 24 withheld**, each of the 24 named with its generator. **45 of 100** notes rows owed; documents last folded at 5.266.0. Gate reads **CLOSED, 1 of 9** — narration. `engine/artifact_audit.js` now reads *"data/abra-tags.js is what build/build_tags_js.js would write from data/tags.json"*, no gaps found; it was a GAP that blocked a commit before the rebuild.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Supersedes.** ~~roughly 85–90 figure-level edits~~ — superseded by **119**, derived. The earlier figure was my own hand estimate in `docs/_reports/2026-09-09-six-zero-zero-scope.md`, a dated findings record which is not rewritten. ~~39 lift / 22 stay~~ — superseded by **40 / 24** from the same derivation.
- **Owed to the next major.** none from this row. **OPEN AND NAMED:** releases cut between 02:54 and 03:55 froze a new `data/tags.json` beside the old `data/abra-tags.js`. Both are frozen SOURCES. Under node this changed nothing — `engine/tags.js:56` requires `tags.json` and the browser copy is only read on the browser path, so the measurements taken in that window stand — but a release re-cut is owed before 6.0.0 rests on a snapshot, and it was not done here because an agent was mid-run and cuts its own.

## [5.270.1] — 2026-09-09 — an ability no legal species can carry is a fact about the regulation, not a row in the roster
- **What changed.** `tests/roster.js`: the artifact's `results` array no longer carries rows tagged `no-legal-carrier`. Will, 2026-09-09: *"if its not in the format then toss it out and stop putting here"*. **The rows go; the count does not** — `scope.out_of_scope` and `scope.out_of_scope_by` are written from the same array BEFORE the filter and are derived every run, so the artifact still says how many were excluded and why. Dropping the count too would be the worse bug: a future session sees the stage fall from 316 rows to 202 and cannot tell whether coverage fell or noise was removed. The rows are tagged at the refusal by `cannot(why, 'no-legal-carrier')`, reached only when the legal-species walk returns empty — so the regulation decides this, not the file.
- **Measured.** **The abilities artifact goes 316 rows → 202**, and `COULD-NOT-STAGE` **158 → 44**, which was always the in-scope number. Verdicts now read 139 FIRED-AND-BOARDS-MATCH / 44 COULD-NOT-STAGE / 14 CONTROL-NOT-QUIET / 5 DEFERRED-BY-OWNER = 202. `scope.out_of_scope` still reads **114 no-legal-carrier**. **The gate is unchanged** — `engine/status.js` reads deliberate roster / abilities PASS at *139 of 202 tested*, exactly as before, and still 1 of 9 clauses failing. Only the abilities stage was affected: moves, items and spine carry 0 out-of-scope rows.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing. No published figure moves — the gate reads the same denominator it read before, which is the point: the 202 was always the real one and 316 was the number being printed at it.
- **Owed to the next major.** none. **Still owed and unchanged by this row:** the 14 CONTROL-NOT-QUIET abilities, where the control arm is itself live and the delta cannot be charged to the entity. Those are the instrument's debt, not the regulation's.

## [5.270.0] — 2026-09-09 — narration batch S: the `-fail` family is four mechanisms, and one of them is the corpse rule for a third time
- **What changed.** `engine/medicham2-browser.js` plus two derived params in `engine/tag_dex.js`; four mechanisms, four new probes, four restore knobs. (1) **A `-fail` the handler writes itself NAMES the move** — `allyswitch.onHit` (`data/moves.ts:326`) and `substitute.onTryHit` (`:18314`) announce their own refusal and return `NOT_FAIL`, so one line reaches the wire carrying the move name and, for the threshold refusal, `[weak]` in field 4; this engine wrote the generic two-field line for both. `tests/probe_fail_names_the_move.js`, knob `MEDI_BARE_FAIL_LABELS=1`. (2) **Champions gives Dire Claw a refusal line mainline does not have** — the mod rewrites the whole secondary (`data/mods/champions/moves.ts:191-209`) and announces on an already-statused target, naming the status when the roll matches it; Tri Attack is NOT overridden and refuses silently. `tests/probe_direclaw_refusal_line.js`, knob `MEDI_NO_PROCEDURAL_REFUSAL_LINE=1`. (3) **Two refusals this engine already made and made in SILENCE** — a Yawn at a statused body and a Leech Seed at a seeded one, each a silent conjunct in a guard rather than a branch with a consequence; the seed road also skipped the authority's 90% accuracy roll, so the test moved BELOW the die. `tests/probe_refusal_this_engine_swallowed.js`, knob `MEDI_SWALLOW_REFUSALS=1`. (4) **A body at 0 HP pays no recoil and says nothing** — `Battle#spreadDamage` opens `if (!target || !target.hp) { retVals[i] = 0; continue; }` (`sim/battle.ts:2102-2105`), the third mechanism wearing the rule batch R found in `cureStatus` and `removeVolatile`. `tests/probe_recoil_on_a_corpse.js`, knob `MEDI_RECOIL_ON_A_CORPSE=1`.
- **Measured.** **Gate NARRATION 33 → 21 of 961**, NARRATION-ONLY **32 → 22 causes / 34 → 22 games**, protocol divergence **37 → 25** — `data/game-differential.json`, release `5381b07ea2fa`, n=961 games, `--steering empirical --arm middle --end-state --games 1200 --team-store data/team-pool-frozen --turns 50`, census 830 rows, pool `0d103fb9fa87`, against release `2c4e125866cc`. **BOARD-MATERIAL 0 of 958, unmoved, checked after BOTH measurements.** Zero transfers at either step. `engine/status.js` reads **1 of 9 gate clauses failing** — NARRATION and nothing else. Census 830 live / 0 missing / 830 probed (`data/mechanics-census.json`); all four roster stages 0 DIFFER and 0 DID-NOT-FIRE with 18/18, 44/44, 36/36 and 17/17 anchors live; `tests/test-engine-diff.js` 6000 agreed / 0 disagreed; `engine/all_mechanics_fire.js` 1313 games, 0 threw.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Supersedes.** ~~gate NARRATION 33 of 961, NARRATION-ONLY 32 causes / 34 games, protocol 37~~ retracted — those were release `2c4e125866cc` and are superseded by 21, 22/22 and 25 on `5381b07ea2fa`. They stood only in `docs/ENGINE.md`'s batch R section, which is a dated record and is not rewritten.
- **Owed to the next major.** `docs/ABRA-whitepaper.md` and `docs/ABRA-technical-docs.md` carry the narration figure; `docs/SUMMARY.md` carries the gate-clause count.

## [5.269.1] — 2026-09-09 — a bibliography is where a document is MOST traceable, and the untraceable census was counting it as the opposite
- **What changed.** `engine/docs_scan.js`: two strips in `figuresIn` — an arXiv id and a DOI are citation IDENTIFIERS, not measurements. `ID_WORD` could not carry it, because its lookbehind demands a SPACE and both are written with a colon; that is the identical reason `§` and `#` were each given a strip here after sitting in `ID_WORD` without ever firing. Three cases added to `LEXING_CASES`, which the file requires in both directions: two that must NOT be read, and a control asserting that a real figure standing beside a citation still IS — without it the strips could be satisfied by discarding the whole line.
- **Measured.** **Untraceable figures 33 → 23, and the documents carrying them 5 → 3** — `node engine/docs_scan.js`. `docs/SLOWKING-whitepaper.md` (7) and `docs/ROLE-FAMILY.md` (2) fall to zero and leave the census entirely; `docs/ABRA-whitepaper.md` drops 1. All ten were reference entries: `arXiv:2007.13544`, `DOI:10.1126/sciadv.adg3256` and five more in one bibliography, plus `arXiv 2304.08272` cited in two other documents. **The figure lexer demonstration goes 7/7 → 10/10 cases, all holding**, and `tests/test-docs-current.js` is unchanged at 33 passed, 0 failed.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Supersedes.** ~~untraceable figures 33 across 5 documents~~ — superseded by **23 across 3**, and the 10 were never figures. Stated in `docs/_reports/2026-09-09-six-zero-zero-scope.md`, a dated findings record which is not rewritten.
- **Owed to the next major.** none from this row. **Named rather than fixed:** 7 of the remaining 23 are figures quoted INSIDE their own WITHDRAWN/CORRECTED notice in `docs/MODELS.md` and `docs/ABRA-whitepaper.md`. They are the record working as designed and need no action at 6.0.0, but they inflate this counter and the retraction registry does not know their values, so exempting them is a design question and was deliberately not attempted here.

## [5.269.0] — 2026-09-08 — narration batch R: five mechanisms, sixteen NARRATION-ONLY causes, and a corpse writes no line
- **What changed.** `engine/medicham2-browser.js`, five mechanisms, five new probes, five restore knobs. (1) **A non-permanent forme revert is SILENT** — Champions' own `clearVolatile` closes with `setSpecies(baseSpecies)`, which emits nothing, and its `formeChange` sets `formeRegression` in the Tera branch ALONE (champions 1 assignment, mainline 2), so Stance Change reaches neither branch; this engine paid the revert through `formeSwap`, whose job is to announce one. `tests/probe_forme_revert_silent.js`, knob `MEDI_FORME_REVERT_ANNOUNCES=1`. (2) **A frozen body killed by its own thawing hit is not cured** — `Pokemon#cureStatus` opens `if (!this.hp || !this.status) return false;` (`sim/pokemon.ts:1680-1681`); `_thawCure` re-read the STATUS and its own comment claimed a corpse carries `'fnt'`, which this engine does not write. `tests/probe_thaw_on_a_corpse.js`, knob `MEDI_THAW_CURES_A_CORPSE=1`. (3) **An ability's clamped stat change still announces at magnitude zero** — `Battle#boost`'s zero branch announces for an Ability effect when the call is `isSecondary || isSelf`, and `effect ||= this.effect` is why Defiant, Competitive and Gooey reach it despite passing `null`; two opt-in sites had never opted in. `tests/probe_ability_zero_boost_line.js`, knob `MEDI_NO_ABILITY_ZERO_BOOST=1`. (4) **The absorbed gift's `-end` on the switch-out road**, which `abRewrite` had implemented for the rewrite road only and whose own comment said the switch road "already empties `_vol` wholesale" — it does, silently. `tests/probe_absorb_gift_end_on_leave.js`, knob `MEDI_NO_ABSORB_GIFT_END_ON_LEAVE=1`. (5) **The shield answers before the ability on the pivot road** — `moveSteps` puts `hitStepTryHitEvent` two steps above `hitStepTryImmunity`, and inside that one event handlers are collected volatiles-before-ability, so Protect speaks and Good as Gold and the Prankster refusal do not. `tests/probe_pivot_shield_before_ability.js`, knob `MEDI_PIVOT_ABILITY_BEFORE_SHIELD=1`.
- **Measured.** NARRATION-ONLY **48 → 32 causes / 50 → 34 games**, gate narration **49 → 33 of 961**, protocol diverged **53 → 37** — `data/game-differential.json`, releases `0c5a4da9c512` → `2c4e125866cc`, n=961 games, `--steering empirical --arm middle --end-state --games 1200 --team-store data/team-pool-frozen --turns 50`, census `0b88d51b3463` (830 rows), pins `de38d17e15a2`, pool `0d103fb9fa87`. **BOARD-MATERIAL 0 of 958 after every one of the four measurements** and ZERO transfers at every step; every prediction hit at the point estimate (`data/verification/_prediction-2026-09-09-narration-batch-R.json`, written before each release was cut). Census **830 live / 830 probed** unchanged (`data/mechanics-census.json`); roster **142 / 139 / 487** with 0 DIFFER and 0 DID-NOT-FIRE and every anchor live (`data/roster.{items,abilities,moves}.json`); `test-engine-diff` **6000 agreed / 0 disagreed** (`data/engine-diff.json`). `engine/status.js` reads **1 of 9 gate clauses fail**, the NARRATION one.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Supersedes.** ~~`data/game-differential.json` "was not rewritten and still holds board-material 77 of 961 and protocol 168"~~ retracted — that sentence stood in `docs/ABRA-whitepaper.md`, `docs/ABRA-technical-docs.md`, `docs/MODELS.md` and `docs/DAMAGE-STAGES.md`, and the four whole-game runs of this batch falsified it. Each of the four blocks now carries a dated SUPERSEDED note in place; the 5.249.0 figures beside it are left standing as the dated record they are. It surfaced as four citation-mismatch rows in `tests/test-docs-current.js` the moment 154 stopped being incidentally present in the rewritten artifact — the clause had been passing on a coincidence, not on the citation.
- **Owed to the next major.** `docs/ABRA-whitepaper.md` and `docs/SUMMARY.md`, wherever the whole-game narration rate is quoted. BOARD-MATERIAL is unmoved at 0 of 958, so nothing that rests on the quarantine bar changes.

## [5.268.1] — 2026-09-08 — the rotation checklist derived its blast radius from files that READ the config, so a typed format id was invisible to it by construction
- **What changed.** `engine/next_regulation.js --checklist` gains a step, and the predicate behind it moves to a new `engine/format_id_scan.js` that `tests/probe_format_id_derivation.js` now requires instead of restating. The checklist derives what a regulation flip touches from files that read `data/regulations.json` or take the format from `champions_sim`. **A file with the id TYPED into it does neither**, so it could not appear in a radius whose whole value is completeness — it keeps working after the flip, about the previous regulation, and reports success. Two defects in the probe fell out of the refactor and are fixed: `execFileSync` ran at the default 1 MB `maxBuffer` and threw `ENOBUFS` on the two largest files it scans, and it ignored git stderr, so a brand-new file and a real git failure were indistinguishable and it was calling the former the latter.
- **Measured.** The red arm is the command own output: before this change `--checklist` named **zero** typed format ids; it now prints **35 live call site(s) across 33 file(s)**, derived at run time from the working tree, naming `gen9championsvgc2026regmb`. `tests/probe_format_id_derivation.js` is unchanged in verdict — **GREEN, 0 live call sites across 233 .js files under `engine/` and `build/`**, which are the gate — and its BEFORE side is now complete at **0 git failures** where it reported 1 and then 3. Reg M-C **does not exist yet**: `engine/next_regulation.js` reads 4 Champions VGC formats from the live authority and none sorts after `gen9championsvgc2026regmb`, so nothing here is owed tonight.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing. No published figure moves — this is a procedure and a scanner, and the one count it prints (35) never existed before.
- **Owed to the next major.** none. The 35 sites are **a decision per site at the flip, not a find-and-replace**: a format id under `tests/` is usually a pin on what was MEASURED, and rewriting those would be editing the record.

## [5.268.0] — 2026-09-08 — narration batch Q: thirteen `ordering` causes are seven mechanisms, and two of them are closed
- **What changed.** `engine/medicham2-browser.js`, two mechanisms. (1) **The smart-target shield line.** `move.smartTarget = false` is a ONE-SHOT on the active move (`data/moves.ts:1008-1013`) and `getSmartTargets` CLEARS the field at target selection when the move cannot split (`sim/pokemon.ts:757-768`), so N shields yield N-1 lines and a dart into a side holding ONE live body announces normally. This engine silenced every refusal. New probe `tests/probe_smart_target_shield_line.js` (four arms, two knobs — `MEDI_SMART_SHIELD_ALL_SILENT=1` is new and gives 0 lines, `MEDI_SMART_PROTECT_LINE=1` gives 2, the authority gives 1). (2) **The `DamagingHit` event.** It is ONE event raised BELOW the secondaries and sorted `onDamagingHitOrder ASC → priority → TARGET INDEX ASC`, never by speed (`data/mods/champions/scripts.ts:374-410`, `sim/battle.ts:789` and `:421`); Cursed Body and Poison Touch were paid inside `_stepEffects`, the secondary step, and are now deferred to a new `_stepDamagingHitLate`. New probe `tests/probe_damaginghit_order.js`, knob `MEDI_DH_IN_EFFECTS=1`. Two dead red-anchors in `tests/roster.js` re-aimed.
- **Measured.** **`ordering` NARRATION-ONLY causes 13 → 10** and **gate narration 52 → 49 of 961** — `data/game-differential.json`, releases `f6f44b329132` → `3b30a88ffa23` → `0c5a4da9c512`, `--steering empirical --arm middle --end-state --games 1200 --team-store data/team-pool-frozen --turns 50`, 961 games, cap 50, census `87d990cf3634` (830 rows), pins `de38d17e15a2`, pool `0d103fb9fa87`. **BOARD-MATERIAL holds at 0 of 958 across both fixes.** Protocol raw 56 → 53. Census **830 live of 830 probed** — `data/mechanics-census.json`. Roster **items 142 / abilities 139 / moves 487, 0 FIRED-AND-BOARDS-DIFFER and 0 DID-NOT-FIRE** on all three stages, re-run on the final release. `data/engine-diff.json` **6000 compared / 6000 agreed / 0 disagreed**; `data/all-mechanics-fire.json` re-published on the same release (1313 games, 0 threw). `engine/status.js` reads **1 of 9 gate clauses fail**, the same NARRATION clause.

- **Supersedes.** ~~gate narration 52 of 961~~ retracted — superseded by **49 of 961** on release `0c5a4da9c512`; it stood only in the row above and in `docs/ENGINE.md`, both of which describe their own dated run and are not restated as current. No living document carries it.
- **Owed to the next major.** none. **Open and named rather than assumed absent:** the residual-order class (3 games) is NOT explained — two deliberate fixtures show the engines agreeing both at different Speeds and at an exact cross-side tie, so the next pass must read those three games' actual Speeds; substitute step 0 (3 games) and the second `eachEvent('Update')` below `faintMessages` (1 game) are diagnosed with source lines and not attempted, both because they can move a board; `_stepDamagingHit` still mixes the order-1 punishers with the default-order ones (1 game). Full account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-08-narration-ordering-3.md`. Still red and untouched, as before: `tests/test-docs-quarantine.js`, `tests/test-pinch-family.js` (1 of 61), `tests/test-quality.js` (1 of 31), and `status.js`'s FEATURE SEMANTICS on `data/policy-weights.json`.

- **What changed.** `engine/analyze.js` filters the usage model on the store's per-row format token and REFUSES to write a model when no stored row is the active regulation. `engine/durable-ingest.js` factors the token derivation out of `extract()` into exported `formatToken()` / `activeStoreFormat()` — the latter deriving the token twice, from the label and from the Showdown id, and throwing when they disagree — and gains a `STALE-LADDER` discriminator so a ROTATED ladder is no longer indistinguishable from a quiet one. `engine/artifact_audit.js` gains check **H**: the format is asked whether every legal `megaStone` forme has a row carrying `bs`, which is the field `buildMon` gates on. `build/build_browser_data.js`, `engine/derive_switch_carry.js`, `engine/fixture_preflight.js` and `engine/mega_harvest.js` take their format through `champions_sim` instead of naming M-B. `engine/next_regulation.js --checklist` prints the flip procedure with its blast radius derived on every run. `.github/workflows/ingest.yml`'s pull-failure message names both discriminators instead of asserting a broken endpoint. New probes: `tests/probe_usage_regulation_pool.js`, `tests/probe_new_mega_row.js`, `tests/probe_rotated_ladder.js`, `tests/probe_format_id_derivation.js`.

Check H reads **76 of 76** legal mega formes present with base stats and `engine/artifact_audit.js` exits 0, no gaps. The extractor refactor re-parses **300 raw logs from `data/games.ladder.raw-logs.jsonl`: 300 agree, 0 disagree** against the stored rows, and `tests/test-parse.js` is 42 of 42. Red arms, each reproduced before the fix and refused after: pooling **2,406 → 2,324 team-sides** (82 sides of Reg M-A, from the 51 real rows in `data/games.gen9championsvgc2026regmabo3.jsonl`, inside a model labelled `gen9championsvgc2026regmb`; top three species moved 0.5–0.6 points); a legal mega with no row **exit 0 with 0 GAP → exit 1 with 1 GAP naming it**; a rotated ladder **exit 0 and silent → exit 1 STALE-LADDER**, with the QUIET control still exit 0 and the DEAD control still owned by ZERO-GAIN; format ids typed into a live call under `engine/` and `build/` **5 at HEAD → 0 in the tree**. The rotation bar is measured, not assumed: the live pool held 1,275 ids, newest 0.02 h old, spanning 22.50 h, and the 24 h floor is set against this store's 40.7 games/hour over its last 14 days.

## [5.268.0] — 2026-09-08 — the post-action re-sort ran above the Update pass, so a mid-turn White Herb handed Unburden its doubling one action too late
- **What changed.** `engine/medicham2-browser.js` — the post-action re-sort `_resortTail(actIdx)` moves from ABOVE `_updateAll()` to BELOW it, which is where the authority puts it: the re-sort is the LAST statement of `Battle#runAction` (`sim/battle.ts:2915-2922`) and `eachEvent('Update')` is at `:2856`, so an `onUpdate` item is spent BEFORE the remaining queue is sorted. A White Herb spent by the Update pass that closes a PIVOT SWITCH therefore reached the sort in Showdown and not here. New counter `MEDSEEN.queueResortBelowUpdate`; `MEDI_RESORT_BEFORE_UPDATE=1` restores the old placement verbatim. `tests/probe_midturn_herb_resort.js` — new, cast derived from `data/tags.json` and the format, three arms (REAL / SILENT / knob-child), every fixture inequality checked against the authority's own `getActionSpeed()` readings. `docs/ENGINE.md` gains the pass and its hand list.
- **Measured.** **`order_probe` rows carrying `speed_tied:false` AND `same_priority:true`: 1 → 0**, and `node engine/quarantine.js --order-probe` **exit 2 → exit 0** — `data/game-differential.json`, releases `7f012a9afe01` → `f6f44b329132`, `--steering empirical --arm middle --games 1200 --team-store data/team-pool-frozen --end-state --write`, census `886d47cf7fef` (830 rows, CREDITED ONLY under empirical steering), pins `de38d17e15a2`, cap 50, n=961 games each. **BOARD-MATERIAL 0 of 958 → 0 of 958**, 10,675 of 10,675 turn boundaries identical. **NARRATION-ONLY 53 → 52 of 961** across 51 causes. The `ordering` class falls **14 → 13 games** with the other thirteen causes byte-identical and nothing new in either direction. `data/mechanics-census.json` **830 live / 830 probed, `run_ok true`, unchanged**. All three roster stages re-run on `f6f44b329132` (`tests/roster.js --stage {items,abilities,moves} --reds --write`): `data/roster.items.json` **142**, `data/roster.abilities.json` **139**, `data/roster.moves.json` **487**, every count identical to the pre-fix run and **0 DIFFER, 0 DID-NOT-FIRE on all three**.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing. The row was declared OPEN and gating; no figure was published from it.
- **Owed to the next major.** none. **Owed now:** (a) `orderProbeClause`'s rerun hint in `engine/quarantine.js` omits `--end-state`, which is sample-defining per `game_differential.js:200-207` — it cost one 13-minute run in this pass and is reported, not changed; (b) 517 speed readings disagree in 223 of 961 games, every one carrying `status=-/sd:fnt` (the authority's body fainted where this engine's is not), parting no board — not investigated and claimed neither way; (c) `ability/priority-mod`'s delivery move is still not learnset-aware. Full account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-08-gap-286-ordering.md`.
- **Correction to a premise, recorded because it was carried into the brief.** Gale Wings was believed *"never once exercised"* on the strength of a `COULD-NOT-STAGE` in `data/roster.abilities.json`. It is covered by **three live rows in `data/mechanics-census.json`**, including the exact full-HP control — *"Gale Wings puts a full-HP Flying move in front, and not a chipped one"* (`full HP 0`, `at 60% 60`) and *"the Gale Wings body ONE HP below full was refused false / landed true"*. A refusal by ONE instrument was read as an absence across all of them; the roster debt is real and is about its delivery table, not about the mechanic.
- **Which engine was wrong, established rather than assumed.** Under the knob the two engines agree on EVERY boundary speed reading (0 disagreeing, 0 desyncs) and the order still parts — so both put the same number in the sort key and medicham2 was wrong about the moment, not the number. Trick Room, Gale Wings and Prankster were each refuted for this row by measurement: Champions NEGATES for Trick Room (`data/mods/champions/scripts.ts:45-53`) and the row's speeds are POSITIVE (664, 378).
- **Honest reading of the green.** `--order-probe` now passes *"clean by absence: 961 games produced NO move-vs-move ordering pair to probe."* The differential proves the ROW is gone; what proves the MECHANIC is the probe, which stages the fixture deliberately and carries a knob-cleared control.

- **What changed.** `engine/medicham2-browser.js` — the per-arrival volley re-price now routes its closure on `hitPlanOf`'s `perHitPower` (which decides which road `dmgRange` takes) instead of on whether the arrivals' bands happened to be equal, and its arrival-0 invariant now asks an absorbed volley to agree that the click is absorbed. `tests/probe_arrival_drift_zero.js` — new, six arms, red via `MEDI_ARRIVAL_REPRICE_BANDROUTE=1`. `tests/roster.js` — Leppa Berry and Shed Shell staged (`PP_DRAIN`, `TRAP_ESCAPE`, `escapeVerdict`); five expired refusal reasons repaired or re-anchored.

- **Supersedes.** Nothing. The 39 drifts were declared OPEN by the 2026-09-07 batch-N report, not published as a figure.
- **Owed to the next major.** none. **Owed now:** the per-arrival crit vector on an ABSORBED volley — found by this pass's Disguise control arm, reading identically under `--red` and therefore **not this batch's**: the authority draws a `|-crit|` on arrival 2 that this engine does not (45 against 30) while the absorb, the bust, the chip and every later arrival agree. Full account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-08-volley-reprice-and-expired-reasons.md`.

It reproduces exactly under the driver that produced it.


## [5.268.0] — 2026-09-08 — the three Protect/Detect orderings are not the tie device, and #376's instrument is red for a pair the row does not describe
- **What changed.** `tests/probe_protect_tie_order.js` — new, and it is the discriminator ROADMAP #376 wrote for itself. **No engine file was edited.** `docs/ENGINE.md` gains the pass and its hand list.

By the row's own branch table 100% means *"these three are something else"*. Board-material **0 of 958, unchanged** — `data/game-differential.json`, release `1415f271058e`, generated 2026-09-08T16:15:01.893Z.

## [5.268.0] — 2026-09-08 — MILTANK does not appear to know speed ties exist, and that is written down rather than fixed
- **What changed.** `docs/MODELS.md` only — a note under MILTANK, on Will's instruction (*"just add it to miltank notes that we can review once medicham is done"*). **No code, no measurement, no figure.**
- **Measured.** NO FIGURE. The supporting evidence is an **absence and is labelled as one**: a grep for `speed tie`, `speedTie`, `tieBranch`, `tie.*branch` across `engine/miltank.js`, `engine/rollout_leaf.js` and `engine/board.js` returns nothing, and `tie` does not appear in `rollout_leaf.js` at all. That says the concept is unnamed in those files, **not** that the behaviour is wrong.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing.
- **Basis.** unchanged — a note records an open question; it moves no published figure.
- **Owed to the next major.** none. **Owed to SEARCH after MEDICHAM:** read the tie resolution path and establish whether a rollout SAMPLES a tie or branches on it — and whether MILTANK's value for that action averages over both orders or over a single draw. **A determinism introduced for the differential (ROADMAP #376) must never leak into the player**, and would be invisible if it did, because a player that always wins ties looks like a player that is doing well.


## [5.268.0] — 2026-09-08 — a line ending could refuse a before/after, and the five clauses the release cut staled all reproduce

- **What changed.** `engine/engine_release.js` gains `sha12Content` — a SECOND digest, sha256 over EOL-normalised bytes, beside `sha12` and never replacing it. `sha12` stays raw bytes because a release id is an IDENTITY and 601 manifests hold raw digests; the file's own header already argues that and is untouched. The four axes that are NOT identities now use the content digest and record the byte digest beside it: `steering.driver_code` (+`files_raw`, `raw_digest`), `steering.input_digest` (+`input_raw_digest`), `steering.alignment_inputs` and `driver_inputs` in `engine/game_differential.js` (+`raw_digest`), and `engine/provenance.js`'s stamped-input check, which stops carrying a fourth copy of the hash. `steering.stampsAgree` gives all four one share-any-value rule so an artifact holding only an old raw digest still meets a new arm. `.gitattributes` pins the 14 non-SOURCE files the derivation named. `tests/probe_instrument_digest.js` gains the RED/GREEN demonstration. Then the five stale gate clauses were re-run on release `1415f271058e`, every command read off `engine/status.js`.
- **Measured.** THE DEFECT, both directions, on files `git status` reports CLEAN: `data/protocol-events.json` `7c9de3868d6f` → `2638eb253525` and `engine/medicham2-browser.js` `9a54ee6881cf` → `8bdea30dbb42`, where the old digest is the sha256 of the LF form of the same bytes — `core.autocrlf` is `true` here and both index blobs are pure LF with **zero lone CRs**. THE POPULATION, derived from the code that computes each digest rather than typed: **41 members, 23 at risk before this pass and 9 after**, and all 9 remaining are the release SOURCES already filed as a separate job in `.gitattributes`. SHOWN RED FIRST, with no typed digest — both byte forms handed to the real producers — `tests/probe_instrument_digest.js` case 7 reads NOT COMPARABLE on the pre-fix producer (`7c9de3868d6f` vs `2638eb253525`, one reason, the ALIGNMENT RULE) and COMPARABLE on this pass's (`7c9de3868d6f` vs `7c9de3868d6f`, raw digests still differing and still recorded); two controls hold — a row removed from the skip list and a moved instrument file are both still refused, the second BY NAME. Case 8: **9 of 16 instrument files differ raw-vs-content on this tree right now**. NO PUBLISHED FIGURE MOVED BY THE DIGEST CHANGE: `provenance` reads **184 UNSAFE, 2 VOID, 39 possibly stale, 30 ok, 0 missing** — `data/provenance-stamp.json` — identical either side of it; it reads **184 / 2 / 35 / 34 / 0** after the five re-runs below, and those four are the re-run artifacts. THE FIVE RE-RUNS, all on release `1415f271058e`: **6000 compared / 6000 agreed / 0 disagreed** (`data/engine-diff.json`, `--n 6000 --seed 20260804`); **items 140 of 148**, **abilities 139 of 202**, **moves 487 of 500**, each with **0 FIRED-AND-BOARDS-DIFFER and 0 DID-NOT-FIRE** (`data/roster.{items,abilities,moves}.json`); and `data/all-mechanics-fire.json` reproduces the pre-cut artifact **field for field** — moves 500 tried / 495 resolved / 4 diverged / 11 resolution disagreements, abilities 316 tried / 104 fired / 1 diverged, items 148 / 64 / 0 — with only the release stamp moving `f0f10cd06861` → `1415f271058e`. Census unchanged at **830 rows**, `tests/test-mechanics.js` deliberately not re-run. BOARD-MATERIAL untouched at **0 of 958**: no whole-game differential was run, so `data/game-differential.json` is the artifact that produced it.
- **Basis.** unchanged. Every re-run is the same question on the same pins with a new release stamp, and the digest change is EOL-normalisation only: it agrees with `sha12` on every LF file, so a digest recorded when a file was LF reproduces exactly.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing new. The `146` roster-abilities figure was already superseded by `139` on 2026-09-08; this pass PROPAGATES that correction into the two hand-authored statements in `docs/ENGINE.md` that had missed it, because the file holds exactly ONE `<!-- GENERATED -->` block and it contains neither number — so `--write` could never have reached them. `:354`'s claim that the count *"is stamped into `<!-- GENERATED -->` blocks and still reads 146 there"* is struck as false on both clauses. `:522`'s `146 of 202` is struck and superseded with `139 of 202`, original left visible, reason stated. `:16998` reads `129 of 202` in a **2026-08-26** section about release `7fc604e5bc44` and is deliberately NOT edited — 129 was the truth on those bytes and rewriting a dated measurement is falsifying it.
- **Owed to the next major.** none. **Named, not fixed:** the nine release SOURCES are still translatable and pinning them still moves every release id and breaks `tests/roster.js`; a legacy artifact stamped raw-CRLF against a new run on an LF checkout is still refused and cannot be repaired after the fact, which case 7 asserts rather than hides; and `tests/test-engine-release.js` and `tests/test-artifact-rerunnable.js` die at exit 134 on node's default heap inside `REL.compat` (601 releases × a 3.2 MB simulator) and pass at `--max-old-space-size=4096` — not caused by this pass, neither is a registered gate. Full account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-08-crlf-digests-and-restale.md`.

## [5.268.0] — 2026-09-08 — the seam change is a no-op for the played sample, and the register regenerated on a quiet tree reproduces itself row for row

- **What changed.** No source file. Three runs on a settled tree (`97d7b389`, clean, no other agent live), taken because two things had been left unmeasured. (1) `engine/champions_sim.js` gained a `dexFor()` refusal and a `Dex` Proxy and was deliberately NOT re-measured, leaving an object-identity proof standing in place of a measurement. (2) `data/register-reality.json` had been rewritten while `engine/medicham2-browser.js` was mid-edit, with no `tree_state` on it or on the twelve-day-old artifact it superseded, so nothing separated a real repair from a torn read. A release was required and was cut: `engine_release.drift(f0f10cd06861)` named three SOURCE files, of which `engine/rollout_leaf.js` (14 lines) and `engine/champions_sim.js` (108 lines) really changed. `node engine/status.js --write` restamped all five ledgers.
- **Measured.** **BOARD-MATERIAL 0 of 958 and NARRATION 53 of 961 across 52 causes** — `data/game-differential.json`, release `1415f271058e`, arm `middle`, `empirical-click/v1`, `--games 1200 --turns 50`, census pin `9446a684709d`, `--team-store data/team-pool-frozen` (pool `0d103fb9fa87`, 8,778 teams, 1,968 picked), driver code `49cc7bf27fb2`, **961 played / 958 readable / 3 void**, 0 games cut off by the cap, planted-divergence proof fired. The bar is `state.games` less `state.games_board_never_diverged`; `by_cause_totals.games_board_material` reads 3 and answers a different question. **Both figures were predicted in writing before the run and both held.** The sample is provably the SAME sample and not merely the same number: the `first_divergences` list is identical game-for-game across all 57, the class table is identical, the `errors` block is the same single pre-existing entry, and `mode`, `pins.digest`, the pool digest and the census pin all match the previous run. **`data/register-reality.json`, regenerated over 73 instruments on the quiet tree, reproduces the mid-edit artifact 123 of 123 verdicts with ZERO changed** — so all **37** verdict changes against the 2026-08-27 baseline (`f8716264`, 112 rows) are real and none was torn; 24 of the 37 are `INSTRUMENT UNRUNNABLE` becoming `CONFIRMED`. Run-level counts: `stale_rows 10`, `premature_closes 3`, `unrunnable 0`, `cannot_answer 0`, `markers_rejected 0`. `tree_state` lands and records `head 97d7b389`.
- **Basis.** unchanged. This is a re-measurement on unchanged pins — same arm, same census pin, same frozen team store, same die model — and no published figure moves. A reader can still be told exactly what they were told before.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing.
- **Owed to the next major.** none. **The gate now reads 7 of 9 clauses failing, and FIVE of the seven are the cost of the release cut rather than a measured disagreement:** `game differential`, `deliberate roster / items`, `/ abilities`, `/ moves` and `mechanics` all read `MEASURED AGAINST A DIFFERENT ENGINE — ran on release f0f10cd06861 and the tree is 1415f271058e`, and each owes a re-run that `status.js` names itself. The two measured failures are NARRATION (53) and `no open, known engine defect` (`#376` is open, asserts breakage, and its instrument exits 1). BOARD-MATERIAL and coverage both PASS on bytes measured today. **Named, not fixed:** two content digests moved for LINE ENDINGS alone and neither file is covered by `.gitattributes` — `engine/medicham2-browser.js` `9a54ee6881cf` to `8bdea30dbb42` with a CR-insensitive diff of **0 lines** (`status.js` detects this and says so in every stale clause), and the alignment axis `data/protocol-events.json` `7c9de3868d6f` to `2638eb253525` on a file git reports CLEAN, which `engine/arms_comparable.js` compares unconditionally; `register_reality.js` cannot ever read `settled: true`, because three of the instruments it runs write tracked artifacts (`data/forme-assert.json`, `data/mechanics-census.json`, `data/provenance-stamp.json` — timestamp and ratchet only on this run); and the stale **146** roster-abilities figure is in NO `<!-- GENERATED -->` block in any of the five ledgers, so the restamp could not reach it — it is hand-authored in `docs/ENGINE.md` (`:522`, `:16998`), which already carries a `146` to `139` heading at `:247`. Full account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-08-settled-tree-runs.md`.

## [5.268.0] — 2026-09-08 — an unknown format id resolved to mainline Gen 9 without throwing, and Reg M-C lands in days

- **What changed.** `engine/champions_sim.js`. `Dex.forFormat(id)` does not throw on a format id the checkout has never heard of — it returns the BASE mod, and every caller reports success. A new resolver `dexFor()` refuses on two derived clauses (`Dex.formats.get(id).exists`, and the resolved `currentMod` matching `/^champions/` — a PREFIX test, because `gen9championsvgc2026regma` carries mod `championsregma` and equality would refuse a real regulation), and `sim().Dex` is now a **Proxy** whose only difference is that `forFormat` is `dexFor`. **Structural, not a scan:** 228 call sites are covered with no edit, including all six the readiness sweep named. 13 further sites in 12 files held a direct Dex while carrying a MOVING format id and were fixed by hand. The `FORMAT` fallback in the same file was a bare `catch (e) {}` and is now loud (`verify().format_fallback`). `sim/package.json` `"^0.11.9"` → `"0.11.11"` — a caret range is not a pin, and 0.11.11 is the ONE published version whose legality has been compared to ours (`data/verification/npm-oracle-2026-09-08/npm-oracle-legality.json`, `LEGAL_SETS_IDENTICAL: true`). Safe to change: the package is not installed in `sim/node_modules` at all today, so no live path resolves it. **`engine/medicham2-browser.js` was not touched and no simulator behaviour changed.**
- **Measured.** NO PUBLISHED FIGURE MOVES. The defect and its control are DERIVED at run time by `tests/probe_unknown_format_refusal.js` against the pinned checkout `20ad99ffc9a5` — they are read off `Dex.forFormat`, not out of an artifact, and the exact counts are in the report. The active format resolves to a champions mod, to the regulation's own legal-species list, with Rocky Helmet and Silk Trap both `isNonstandard: 'Past'`. The first unavailable regulation id resolves to `currentMod=base` — the whole National Dex, and **both banned items legal**. The change is a no-op for the live format by object identity: `CS.sim().Dex.forFormat(CS.FORMAT) === rawDex.forFormat(CS.FORMAT)` is **true**, so every consumer receives literally the same dex instance it received before.
- **RED FIRST.** `tests/probe_unknown_format_refusal.js` — **3 assertions FAILED, exit 1** before the fix (the seam returned a dex for the unavailable regulation id, for a nonsense id, and for the empty string); **13 of 13 GREEN, exit 0** after. The end-to-end clause was written after the seam was wired, and is red without the fix too (`CS.dexFor` does not exist), so **4 of 13** is the honest red count for the probe as it now stands. The control is in-band and never moved: the live regulation resolves, to a champions mod, to the regulation's own species list, with both bans intact. Plus an end-to-end rehearsal in a child process — `CS.FORMAT` moved to the unavailable id, `engine/tag_dex.js` loaded, **REFUSED**. **Nothing hardcodes the id:** the fixture walks the active regulation's token forward and takes the first one the checkout does not carry, so it walks past that format the day Showdown ships it.
- **Basis.** unchanged. No published figure is affected; this closes a hole that would have moved every figure at once, silently, the day `active` moves in `data/regulations.json`.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing.
- **Owed to the next major.** none. **NOT re-measured and deliberately so:** the whole-game differential and `data/mechanics-census.json` were both left alone — two agents were writing `engine/rollout_leaf.js`, `engine/register_reality.js` and `tests/roster.js` within 48 minutes of this run, so an unpinned run would have been a measurement taken beside a writing agent, and a `--release`-pinned run serves the frozen `champions_sim.js` and cannot see the change. The last board-material value stands and is not restated. **Named, not fixed:** 37 call sites in 35 files still pass the literal `'gen9championsvgc2026regmb'` to a direct Dex; they do not follow the active regulation, so they cannot switch dex silently, and they are on the ENGINE hand list. **One thing for whoever owns it:** a smoke run of `engine/format_audit.js` produced a `data/format-audit.json` substantially SHORTER than the committed copy (the diff size is in the report); the file was restored with `git checkout --`, but the committed copy and a fresh derivation disagree. Nothing was committed. Full account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-08-unknown-format-refusal.md`.

## [5.268.0] — 2026-09-08 — the ability-swap control was announcing its own name, and twelve roster greens rested on nothing else

- **What changed.** One instrument, no engine mechanic. `engine/board_state.js` writes a body's ability onto TWO board leaves — the active slot and the party row — so `tests/roster.js`'s ability control arm, which SWAPS the ability, cannot be played without moving them, and `armDelta` counted that as the entity's evidence. It is the Focus Sash defect of 2026-09-07 on the ability axis. `controlOf` now records the swap it performed; `swapLeaf` drops an `.ability` leaf **only** when it reads `with=<the ability under test> without=<that arm's control ability>`; and `DKEY` normalises the control-arm value on a leaf the entity itself rewrote, so a real ability change can survive a second control. **Conditioned on the VALUES, never on the path** — a blanket path ignore would delete Trace's only evidence. Four new rules were added above `ability/entry`, each matching exactly one legal ability by handler shape (`onDeductPP`; `onTryHit` naming `isAlly(`; `onDamage` comparing `effectType` to `"Move"`; `onDamage` naming the poison ids), each shown `CAUGHT` by its own `--reds` break. **`engine/medicham2-browser.js` was not touched and no simulator fix was needed.**
- **Measured.** `data/roster.abilities.json`, release `f0f10cd06861`, `--stage abilities --reds --write`, 316 rows / 202 in scope, previous artifact kept at `data/roster.abilities.prev.json`. **FIRED-AND-BOARDS-MATCH 146 → 139**, **CONTROL-NOT-QUIET 45 → 14**, **COULD-NOT-STAGE 124 → 158**, **DEFERRED-BY-OWNER 1 → 5**, **FIRED-AND-BOARDS-DIFFER 0 → 0** and **DID-NOT-FIRE 0 → 0**. Red demonstrations **44 of 44 CAUGHT, 0 NOT CAUGHT, 0 WEAK**. `swap_leaf_correction.self_describing_dropped` **5,344**, `real_ability_rewrite_kept` **48** — both published in the artifact and printed by `engine/status.js` in its SCOPE block. The grouping, off the pre-fix artifact: **45 of 45** unattributable rows had a control arm whose entire delta was the swap; **1,380 of 1,738** dropped leaves (79.4%) were that leaf; **12 of the 146** greens had no other evidence at all. **No engine defect was found.**
- **RED FIRST, WITH THE KNOB SHOWN REACHING THE RULE.** `tests/probe_control_self_name.js`, seven clauses: **RED 2 of 7** before (Leaf Guard counted as tested on 8 leaves that were all the swap; Trace unattributable), **GREEN 7 of 7** after. Two silent controls in-band — Contrary keeps its green on 24 real leaves **with the correction firing on its row too** (16 dropped), and Trace must come out `FIRED-AND-BOARDS-MATCH` on `p2.active[0].ability with=pressure`, which a blanket ignore would delete. Clause A0 reads the published counter rather than the row, because after the fix the row's evidence is empty and the old clause would have been vacuous; a MISSING counter throws instead of reading as zero. **The correction was itself wrong first and the counter caught it in two minutes**: `swapLeaf` read `d.with`/`d.without` where `BS.compare` writes `.medicham`/`.showdown`, so every leaf classified as 0 and the run printed `0 leaves DROPPED` with its own ZERO warning.
- **Basis.** unchanged. A reader can still be told the same thing — the ability roster's clean count — and told that it went 146 to 139 because twelve of the 146 were measured with a ruler that was describing itself.
- **Supersedes.** ~~roster abilities 146 of 202 tested~~ retracted — **12 of those 146 were vacuous** (Cloud Nine, Leaf Guard, Light Metal, Long Reach, Magician, Natural Cure, Plus, Quick Feet, Receiver, Symbiosis, Synchronize, Unaware) and are now `COULD-NOT-STAGE`. The live figure is **139 of 202**, read from `data/roster.abilities.json`. The superseded 146 appears in no living document — it is stamped only into `<!-- GENERATED -->` blocks, which `node engine/status.js --write` restamps; that command was NOT run here and the restamp is owed. Five rows moved the other way and are newly measured for the first time: **Pressure, Telepathy, Magic Guard, Poison Heal, Trace**. **Four owner deferrals were hidden by the same leaf** — Anticipation, Forewarn, Pickup and Stall — because the shelf branch is gated on `!sdMoved.length`; that was NOT predicted and the miss is recorded in `data/verification/_prediction-2026-09-08-control-not-quiet.json`.
- **Owed to the next major.** `node engine/status.js --write` owes the restamp of the abilities count in the generated blocks. **Named, not fixed:** 14 rows remain `CONTROL-NOT-QUIET`, each now with a sharp diagnosis (one arm at exactly zero, the other arm's leaves belonging to a live control — Intimidate on four of them), and 34 abilities need a fixture that actually reaches them; both lists are enumerated in the report. `data/roster.items.json` and `data/roster.moves.json` were NOT re-run and are provably unaffected — `swapLeaf` returns 0 immediately when the control wrote no ability. `data/mechanics-census.json` was not regenerated: no engine behaviour changed. Nothing was committed. Full account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-08-control-not-quiet.md`.

## [5.268.0] — 2026-09-08 — a frozen release could not serve the leaf's own census and played a switchless game while reporting success, and `--only` was never a flag at all

- **What changed.** Two instruments, no engine mechanic. (1) **`data/rollout-switch-census.json` is now a frozen release SOURCE** (`engine/engine_release.js`, plus `.gitattributes text eol=lf`, which the existing CRLF-stranding clause of `tests/test-engine-release.js` named as owed the moment a 27th LF source appeared). `engine/rollout_leaf.js census()` opens it with `fs` against its own `__dirname`, so a copy loaded out of `data/releases/<id>/engine/` found nothing and took its documented fallback — **`switchRate: 0`, so the playout cannot switch, and a horizon of 60 instead of the store-measured 14** — on one line of stderr. `requireClosure` walks static `require()` edges and says in its own header that it cannot see an `fs.readFileSync`, so the cut was never refused. **This is the fifth growth of SOURCES and the first of the five that did not crash.** Because frozen bytes cannot be repaired, `open().require()` now REFUSES any `.js` source whose snapshot lacks a data file those bytes open — derived from the snapshot's own bytes and intersected with SOURCES, never typed — with `{ dataMissingOk: [...] }` as the explicit opt-out that `engine/bench_speed.js` declares. `engine/rollout_leaf.js` COUNTS the fallback (`SWITCH_COUNTERS.censusFallback`), which `engine/miltank.js` stamps into every decision row. `engine_release.js census()` gains a `data-not-frozen` cause, because it was answering `serviceable` about a release `REL.require` was throwing at. (2) **`engine/register_reality.js --only` is implemented, refuses to publish, and unknown argv is refused.**
- **Measured.** **NO PUBLISHED FIGURE RESTS ON THE CENSUS FALLBACK AND NOTHING IS WITHDRAWN.** Over the **600** release directories on disk: **485** hold a `rollout_leaf.js` that reads the census, **0** carry it. Only four live callers can reach a snapshot leaf; `engine/bench_speed.js` handles it explicitly, and the other three wrote four artifacts — `data/rollout-r1.json` (2026-08-04), `data/rollout-r1-explore-sweep.json` (release `3932186b59ef`), `data/leaf-position-contrast.json` (`6b0e4117d964`) and `data/leaf-engine-contrast.json` (`cf6a68fa412c`/`dc3c43336539`). Every one of those snapshots contains **0** occurrences of `switchRate`: the census reader entered the leaf at commit `f8f2c67f` on 2026-08-10, three to six days after those runs, so a switchless playout was not a degradation there — it was the player. `engine/argmax_paired.js` and SEARCH's decision-profile driver compile the leaf under its LIVE filename and resolve correctly (`data/search-decision-profile.json` records `"turns":14`, not 60). **Adding the census to SOURCES strands nothing**, measured over a six-release probe store: `sources_now` 26 → 27, `runnable` **4 → 4**, causes row for row identical — no caller `REL.require`s a data file, so it never enters the requirement union. `--only 273,449` now runs **2 instruments, not 73**, and leaves `data/register-reality.json` identical to the byte and to the mtime.
- **RED FIRST ON BOTH, WITH THE KNOB SHOWN REACHING THE RULE.** Census, two arms on release `f0f10cd06861`: snapshot `{switchRate:0, maxTurns:0, ok:false}` against live `{switchRate:0.0998, maxTurns:14, ok:true}` — a success-shaped object nothing downstream refused. After the fix the snapshot arm THROWS and names the file. `tests/test-engine-release.js` §11 carries it in-band, deriving its RED release rather than naming one, and reads **80 passed, 0 failed** (was 71): RED refuses, **CONTROL** with `dataMissingOk` declared loads the same release, GREEN cuts a release that carries the census. The counter has a silent control — `fs.readFileSync` stubbed for that one path gives `censusFallback=1`, unstubbed gives `0`. `--only`: `grep -c -- "--only"` on the pre-fix bytes returned **0**, and `--list` printed byte-identical output with and without it; after the fix `--onyl` exits 2, a value-less or non-numeric `--only` exits 2, and the selftest reads **82 passed, 0 failed** (was 73) with two CONTROL arms — a parser that refused everything and a fixture with nothing to filter would both pass the refusal assertions alone. `tests/test-artifact-rerunnable.js` unchanged at `1 known, was 1`.
- **Basis.** unchanged. No figure this project has published was produced through the census fallback, so no reader has to be told that a number answered a different question. What moved is future work: every quarantined re-run — leaf calibration, R1–R4, the rollout figures — loads the leaf out of a frozen release, and with this in place each would have been a switchless playout at a horizon five times too long, announcing success.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing is retracted. **One structural figure in a living document has stopped being true and is named rather than rewritten in place:** `docs/ABRA-technical-docs.md:791` says *"the hash of the bytes of 26 frozen sources"* and line 812 *"17 of the 26 … the other 9 have CRLF bytes on the disk today"*; the live values are **27, 18 and 9**, read from `REL.SOURCES` and printed by `tests/test-engine-release.js` on every run. Both sit inside a dated 2026-08-26/28 fault narrative, which this project does not edit in place. **No quarantined figure is read or quoted here.**
- **NOT DONE, AND THE REASON IS MEASURED.** `data/register-reality.json` on disk holds **123 rows from a full 73-instrument run taken while `engine/medicham2-browser.js` was being edited**, superseding a **twelve-day-stale** 112-row artifact, with **37 verdicts changed** and no `tree_state` on either — so nothing in either file separates "twelve days of real repair" from a torn read. **Those rows are NOT QUOTABLE and none is quoted anywhere in this pass.** It was not regenerated: `tests/roster.js` was modified **17 seconds** before that check, `node tests/roster.js --stage moves` is one of the 73 instruments, and a run started then would have manufactured a second torn artifact by the same mechanism wearing a fresh timestamp. It was not hand-edited either — `publish()` is the only write site and going round it with an editor is the manufactured-receipt shape. The repair is `node engine/register_reality.js` over a settled tree, about 28 minutes summed from the artifact's own per-instrument `ms`, and the result will now carry `tree_state.settled`.
- **Owed to the next major.** `docs/ABRA-technical-docs.md` owes the 26 → 27 correction at lines 791 and 812. **Filed, not done:** `tests/test-artifact-rerunnable.js judge()` does not apply the new data-dependency rule — it is not wrong today, because the only twelve artifacts that would change band are `bench_speed.js`'s and it declares the opt-out, but closing it needs `callerNeeds()` to parse `dataMissingOk`. **Reported, not touched:** `tests/probe_control_self_name.js` and `data/verification/_prediction-2026-09-08-control-not-quiet.json` are another division's untracked work-in-progress. `node engine/status.js --write` was NOT run and no version was bumped. Full account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-08-census-in-release-and-only-flag.md`.

## [5.268.0] — 2026-09-08 — the 8.8x was two numbers that never answered the same question, and the loss that is real is mechanics arriving at one unit of cost per unit of code

- **What changed.** No engine byte and no published figure. A BISECTION of MEDICHAM's throughput across the frozen release store, running `data/verification/speed-2026-09-08/bench_two_engines.js` **unmodified** — one process per release, 47 release points, five passes alternating forward and reverse — plus a CPU profile of the oldest and newest openable engines, a counter-write-rate probe, and a fit of cost against simulator size. New files, all under `data/verification/speed-bisect-2026-09-08/`: `run_pass.sh`, `collate.js`, `noise.js`, `prof_summary.js`, `prof_diff.js`, `instrumentation_rate.js`, `size_vs_speed.js`, `make_proto_pins.js`, `nan_counter.js` and their artifacts.
- **Measured.** **THE ENGINE IS 2.19x SLOWER THAN ON 2026-08-12** — normalised cost `0.1345 -> 0.2942` across 47 resolved releases, `data/verification/speed-bisect-2026-09-08/curve.json`, noise floor **7.3%**, 225 of 470 legs clean. Two instruments that share nothing but the pool agree: an uncontended 400-playout probe reads `0.3403 -> 0.7327 ms/turn` (**2.15x**, `instrumentation-rate-*.json`) and the CPU profile reads MEDICHAM-side self time `891 -> 1,745 ms` (**1.96x**) on the same 100-game sample. **NO STEP IS A CLIFF:** the largest interval step is **+11.2%** across two commits against a 7.3% floor, and three of the six biggest steps are inside the floor. `size-vs-speed.json`: `log(cost) = 0.945 * log(non-comment bytes)`, **R^2 0.934, n = 47** — cost grows almost exactly in proportion to code.
- **THE 8.8x IS NOT A MEASURED QUANTITY AND IS NOT DEFENDED HERE.** Its 2026-08-06 endpoint has a same-day sibling that disagrees with it by **4.06x** — commit `e29a26d9` records 3,212 turns/sec at **2.0 turns per battle** and commit `2fb0928a`, five hours later on the same engine, records 13,041 at **60.1 turns per battle**, i.e. every battle running to the cap. Neither committed a script (`git show --stat` on both). Today's runs play 10.1-11.6 turns and reach a result in **100%** of games. Extrapolating the fitted law across the window that cannot be opened gives **~6.0x** for 2026-08-06 -> 2026-09-08 — an extrapolation, labelled as one, never to be quoted as a measurement.
- **THE LEADING HYPOTHESIS IS REFUTED BY THE PROFILE.** The shared dice addressing — the `seed|turn|cat|move|target|nth` string, the repeat map and the FNV-1a+fmix32 hash — is built by `game_differential.js` for the middle arm and **never runs inside `runPlayout`**, which hands `rngStreams()` a plain function and takes the documented back-compatible path. What the play path pays is the address FIELD WRITES: `_midWriteActionAddr` + `midEventSlot` + `rngStreams` + `midAbortTwoTurn` = **12.7 ms of 1,745, or 0.73%**. The other three candidates are confirmed and are all mechanics — the residual family (`_updateEvent`, `_walk`, `residualExpireAt`, `residualOrder`, `residualFollowerRuns`, `residualClockTick`) is **13.7% of the rise**, `dmgRangeOneHit` is **2.37x**, `effSpeed` is **3.42x**.
- **THE INSTRUMENTATION / MECHANICS SPLIT.** Measured rates per turn on release `cf8567c4db78`: **149.6** `MEDSEEN` writes, 0.15 `MEDFAILS`, **1,454.1** `tags.ASKED` (one per `param()`/`has()` call) and **1,490.9** `tags.COUNT`, at a micro-benchmarked **17.4 / 17.0 ns** per write — **52.7 us of 733 us per turn, 7.2%**. With the address writes and the protocol trace that is **~9%**: stripping every counter and every address field from a play build takes 1,365 turns/sec to about **1,490**. **The other ~90% is the engine modelling the game and is not recoverable.** One lever sits in neither bucket and is NAMED, NOT FIXED: `engine/tags.js` is **15.7% of MEDICHAM self time at 1,454 lookups per turn**, and `norm()` — an uncached `toLowerCase()` plus regex strip on every one of them — is **5.6% of the whole engine**; that is ENGINE's file, it is a frozen release SOURCE, and it needs its own attributable change.
- **THE LIVE TREE MOVED UNDER THIS MEASUREMENT AND A THROW CAUGHT IT.** `data/protocol-events.json` was regenerated at **05:36Z mid-pass**, and `game_differential.js`'s alignment guard then refused **25 of 47** processes at module load. Pass 1's numbers are unaffected — the benchmark compares no protocol and reads neither engine's log — and the remaining passes ran against per-release load-guard pins written by `make_proto_pins.js`, each stamped `_synthetic` and each stating it is **not** an alignment rule. The pin is inert by construction and the releases measured both ways agree inside the floor.
- **RELEASE STORE, REPORTED NOT FIXED.** Of **599** releases, **4** carry no engine bytes at all (`5fc1f711a0e3`, `2bddcc69ca83`, `4c79682a3115`, `55c7a0f19c86` — the checksum era), **1** is ROT (`63e0e063bfed` holds `0608fb84f841` where its own manifest says `a13b89d2689f`), and **172** predate `spreadL50`/`rngStreams` so today's `game_differential.js` refuses them by name — `docs/LESSONS.md` §12, the reader moving rather than the snapshot rotting. The whole 2026-08-04 -> 2026-08-12 window, where both disputed historical figures were taken, is therefore **unmeasurable with the instrument that produced today's figure**, and is stated as such rather than estimated into the curve. Also found in passing: `MEDSEEN.retaliateWhenLowered` goes **NaN** during ordinary play on release `05e03b600fc7`, so that release's counter rate could not be summed; it is finite on `cf8567c4db78`.
- **Basis.** unchanged. This row publishes no new figure about the engine's correctness and supersedes no series; it measures a quantity — MEDICHAM's throughput over time — that had one artifact and two undocumented readings.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing is retracted. `13,041` and `3,212` turns/sec are **kept as what they were**: correct readings of an engine that could not finish a battle, taken with methods recorded in their commit messages and nowhere else. What this row adds is that **they cannot be divided into today's number**, so `8.8x` should not be repeated as a slowdown; the defensible statements are **2.19x measured since 2026-08-12** and **~6.0x extrapolated since 2026-08-06**. `data/medicham-speed.json` (2026-08-28, 1,596-2,044 turns/sec) is consistent with this curve's 1,513 at `345f4193d440` and is not superseded. No quarantined figure is read or quoted; R1, R2, R3, R4 and leaf calibration stay withheld — throughput says nothing about any of them.
- **Owed to the next major.** Nothing published moves, so nothing is owed to the documents by this row. **Named and not fixed, for the divisions that own them:** the `tags.js` lookup path above is ENGINE's; the ROT release `63e0e063bfed` and the four byte-less releases are OPS/MEASURE housekeeping and are left in place, not deleted; and **MEDICHAM's turns/sec still is not on the status board next to its mechanics count**, which is the same recommendation `docs/_reports/2026-09-08-engine-speed-comparison.md` made and the reason nobody noticed a 2.19x drift — this bisect is what it costs to reconstruct a number that could have been one line of `status.js`. `node engine/status.js --write` was NOT run: an ENGINE agent holds it. Full account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-08-speed-regression-bisect.md`.

## [5.268.0] — 2026-09-08 — the declared skip list was in no digest set, and fifteen "shown red before trusted" certificates had not run since the wires that stranded them

- **What changed.** No engine byte; two instruments. (1) **`data/protocol-events.json` — the DECLARED SKIP LIST — is now an ALIGNMENT INPUT.** It decides which Showdown lines are deleted before comparison, so it decides what may count as a divergence, and it was in NO digest set: absent from the release's SOURCE files, from `steering.driver_code.files` (it is read with `readFileSync`, so `requireClosure` cannot see it), from `driver_inputs` and from `pins`, while `data/game-differential.json` recorded `driver_code_stable: true`. It landed where `docs/_reports/2026-09-06-comparability-backfill-triage.md` §5 derived it should — `steering.resolve()` beside `driver_inputs`, compared **UNCONDITIONALLY** in `comparable()` rather than inside `TABLE_DRIVEN` (which would skip the 34 coverage-arm artifacts), **absence reading `unknown`** rather than being added to `vouches()` (which would flip 112 artifacts from UNKNOWN to NOT COMPARABLE and assert something false about them). `engine/game_differential.js` gains `--protocol-events <path>` and three REFUSALS; `engine/arms_comparable.js`'s limits line is now COMPUTED from the two blocks in front of it instead of reading `Not stamped.` forever. **The list was also REFIT, not restamped** — `derive_protocol_events.js --write` re-derived it against the current engine. (2) **`tests/probe_red_demo.js`** — fifteen stale reversals re-aimed, one genuine hollow row re-aimed, one accounting defect fixed, and the file WIRED into `tests/run-all.js` as a gate.
- **Measured.** **THE REFIT MOVED NO PUBLISHED FIGURE AND NARRATION DID NOT MOVE.** The whole artifact is byte-identical outside `generated`, `source_digests` and `derivedFrom`: `emitted` 44, `notEmitted` 50, `partial` 10, `events` 91 rows and both gate arrays all unchanged (`git diff --stat data/protocol-events.json` → 3 insertions, 3 deletions). The reason is measurable rather than lucky: the artifact's only dependence on the simulator is `medicham2.TRACE_EVENTS`, and that list is **unchanged between 2026-08-26 and 2026-09-08 — zero events added, zero removed, 44 both times**, on an engine whose digest moved `1337ff095e92` → `5a86b1d52bd5` → `9a54ee6881cf` across the same period. `tests/probe_red_demo.js` reads **200 demonstrations, 0 HOLLOW, 0 COULD NOT BE APPLIED, 2 not in this format, `ABRA-EXIT 0 VERDICT-GREEN`**, from **15 stale and 2 counted hollow** at the start of the pass — `engine/medicham2-browser.js` asserted at `9a54ee6881cf` and `data/abra-tags.js` at `d144f744d2af` before AND after that run, and this session held the file, so the read is not torn. `tests/test-empirical-driver.js` GREEN 26 of 26. `tests/run-all.js --list` reads **27 engine gates** and `RUN tests/probe_red_demo.js`.
- **RED FIRST ON BOTH, WITH SILENT CONTROLS, AND THE KNOB IS PRINTED REACHING THE RULE.** The alignment guard: a byte-identical copy of the skip list at a different path exits **0** (so it is not refusing on the path), while one event removed from `emitted` exits **1** naming the event, a zeroed derivation digest exits **1**, and a replaced Showdown commit exits **1**. `arms_comparable.js`: identical `alignment_inputs` → **COMPARABLE** and the limits line reads *"is CHECKED for this pair"*; one digest changed → **NOT COMPARABLE — shown to differ**; one arm unstamped → **UNKNOWN** and the limits line flips itself. `probe_red_demo.js`: a no-op reversal → `1 HOLLOW`, `ABRA-EXIT 1`; an unmatchable anchor → `1 COULD NOT BE APPLIED`, `ABRA-EXIT 2`; restored → `ABRA-EXIT 0`. **Before the accounting fix the second of those would have exited 1**, i.e. a refusal published as a measured engine defect, which is exactly how ROADMAP #273 was once published as a broken simulator.
- **THE GUARD ASKS ABOUT THE FEATURE FUNCTION, NOT THE STAMP, AND A MEASUREMENT DECIDED THAT.** The triage's proposed diff throws when the skip list's recorded `engine/medicham2-browser.js` digest differs from the release's. During this pass the engine moved **twice** with `TRACE_EVENTS` byte-identical, so that guard would have refused two runs whose alignment rule had not moved at all, on the day it was written — an over-firing gate is the one people learn to ignore. The three clauses that DO throw are the three things the artifact is a function of: the engine's CLAIM (`PROTO.emitted` against the played release's `TRACE_EVENTS`), the DERIVATION RULE (`derive_protocol_events.js`, which holds every declared reason as typed text), and the AUTHORITY (`showdown_pinned_commit`). The stamp is still RECORDED — `derived_from_medicham2` beside `medicham2_played` — so a difference is never silent; it just does not stop a run.
- **Basis.** unchanged. No series moved: the refit reproduced the skip list exactly, and the demonstration file's count answers the same question it has always answered.
- **Supersedes.** ~~`tests/probe_red_demo.js`'s count is WITHHELD — four reads gave 15, 10, 9 and 8 COULD NOT BE APPLIED on a tree that did not move, so every one of them is torn~~ superseded by a settled read, not retracted: that withholding was correct on the evidence it had, and the count is now stated **because the tearing condition is gone** — this session held the file, and the engine and tag digests are asserted identical either side of the run. ~~2 HOLLOW is stable across all four reads~~ **corrected**: only ONE of those was a hollow row. The other was `ROADMAP #81 WIRE 10 the single-target CONTROL`, which did `ran++; failures++; stale.push(...)` and so sat in the HOLLOW count and the COULD-NOT-BE-APPLIED list at once; three more sites outside `demoSource` did the same and all four are fixed. Also ~~`data/protocol-events.json` ... Not stamped~~ retracted from `engine/arms_comparable.js`'s limits list, which now computes that line. No quarantined figure is read or quoted; R1, R2, R3, R4 and leaf calibration stay withheld.
- **Owed to the next major.** Nothing published moves. **The cost is stated rather than left to be discovered:** every artifact on disk predates `alignment_inputs`, so `arms_comparable.js` reads **UNKNOWN** on that axis for every pair drawn from them, and `game_differential.js --baseline <old artifact>` now exits 3 — the same cost `driver_code` imposed on 2026-09-05, and the same honest answer, that the axis was never recorded. Three controls went vacuous the moment the axis existed and were repaired in the same pass (`tests/test-empirical-driver.js`, `tests/test-pin-arms.js`, `tests/probe_instrument_digest.js`); two of them carry the 2026-09-05 pass's comment directly above the line this pass edited, which is the pattern to expect the next time an axis is added. **Named and not fixed, for the divisions that own them:** `tests/probe_red_demo.js`'s anchors are literal source strings and go stale on the next wire by construction — 15 of 200 in thirteen days — so the count belongs on a screen rather than in a comment; and the `WIRE 7 Sitrus` certificate now has **four** engine sites delivering one observable, so it hollows again on the fifth. `node engine/status.js --write` was NOT run — another agent holds it — so the generated blocks in `docs/MEASURE.md` are one restamp behind. **TWO THINGS THIS PASS DID THAT IT SHOULD NOT HAVE, BOTH REPORTED RATHER THAN QUIETLY LEFT.** (1) `engine/register_reality.js --only 273` DID NOT HONOUR `--only`: it ran **73 distinct instruments** over 27 minutes and rewrote `data/register-reality.json` from `112 rows / 2026-08-27T20:06:53Z` to `123 rows / 2026-09-08T06:44:52Z` — ROADMAP #369's shape through a flag that narrows the REPORT and not the WORK. The #273 / #449 rows in it are valid and corroborate this row independently (`CONFIRMED, exit 0, 47,065 ms`, on bytes whose mtime 02:16:17 precedes the process's 02:17:03 start), **but the other 71 were taken beside live writing agents and are not photographs — do not quote them.** Nothing is lost; the previous artifact is `git show HEAD:data/register-reality.json`, and it was deliberately not restored with `git checkout --`. (2) `tests/test-docs-quarantine.js` is **RED, and was red at HEAD before this pass**: two rows of this page state figures out of `data/search-decision-profile.json`, which `engine/quarantine.js` withholds — the MILTANK decision-profile row and the `5.267.0` row, neither written here (`git show HEAD:docs/RUNNING-NOTES.md | grep -n search-decision-profile` → HEAD lines 84 and 93). It is NOT filed as a known failure: the figures have to come OUT of those two rows, which is a rewrite of another division's log entries. This row was checked against the same gate and adds no offending location. Full account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-08-skiplist-refit-and-red-demo.md`.

## [5.267.0] — 2026-09-08 — a residual expiry queues a `|faint|` nothing announces, and the handler that should pay for it is the one at the same order

- **What changed.** `engine/medicham2-browser.js`: at the close of the residual body loop, a body whose Perish Song counter SURVIVES now drains the faint queue, which is `fieldEvent`'s `this.faintMessages()` at `sim/battle.ts:565` reached through the callback branch. The duration-expiry branch `continue`s past that line (`:514-524`), so a counter that reaches zero prints `perish0`, queues a `|faint|` and announces nothing — and the next handler to reach `:565` pays it, inside the same order-24 group. `RESIDUAL_AFTER_PERISH` already models every handler BELOW `perishsong@24.2` and excludes `r === perish`, correctly, so the handler at the SAME order was the only one that could not pay; and the drain it does run is at the FOOT of the walk, which cannot put a `|faint|` BETWEEN two `perishN` lines. Knob `MEDI_RESIDUAL_FAINT_AT_GROUP_END=1` restores. New probe `tests/probe_residual_faint_flush.js`, shown RED on two arms before any engine byte moved, with a knob-cleared over-fire control (`CTRL-NONE`) that agrees on both arms and both engines. **`drainFaints` emits lines and writes no state** — `queueFaint` has already written `curHP`, `fainted` and the faint sequence — so no board can move by construction. Also corrected in place, not deleted: `_stepBuffOnHit`'s header claimed its order against `_stepDamagingHit` was unobservable because *"a body holds one ability"*, which silently assumes ONE body and is false of a spread hit; the pinned pool refutes it on three cards.
- **Measured.** `ordering` **16 → 14 games**, narration raw **56 → 54**, protocol-parted **59 → 57**, `by_cause` rows **57 → 55**, board-material **0 of 958 → 0 of 958** — `data/game-differential.json`, 961 games played / 958 readable / 3 void, release `fb0058fb5702` → `cf8567c4db78` (re-cut `f0f10cd06861` after a comment-only change, which reproduced every figure exactly). Pins on every run: `--steering empirical --arm middle --end-state --state --census data/verification/census-pin-9446a684709d.json --games 1200 --turns 50 --team-store data/team-pool-frozen`; `--games` and `--turns` are part of the SAMPLE and are recorded for that reason. Verdicts identical across all three runs (SAME-END-STATE 958, DIFFERENT-END-STATE 2, THREW 1) and every other divergence class unmoved to the game. `node engine/status.js` clause: **NARRATION-ONLY 55 of 961 → 53 of 961**, gate **8 of 9 PASS** before and after. Prediction written BEFORE the run — `data/verification/_prediction-2026-09-08-batchP-residual-faint-flush.json` — **hit at the point estimate on all seven clauses, with zero transfers.** Census regenerated and level: **830 live / 830 probed / 0 missing**, `data/mechanics-census.json`. The five artifacts the release cut invalidated were re-run and are clean: `data/engine-diff.json` 0 of 6000 at the midpoint and all 14 interior indices; `data/roster.{items,abilities,moves}.json` at 140/148, 146/202 and 487/500 with 0 DIFFER, 0 DID-NOT-FIRE and 0 NOT CAUGHT; `data/all-mechanics-fire.json` 1313 games, 0 threw.
- **THE CONTROLLED COMPARISON IS NAMED, BECAUSE THE INSTRUMENT MOVED MID-BATCH.** `steering.driver_code` went `4dd225b7e8c9` → `81b6bca47dab` between the second run and the third, on `engine/game_differential.js` and `engine/steering.js`, which another agent was editing in the live tree. The before/after above rests on the pair that SHARE `4dd225b7e8c9` — the batch-O baseline and the `cf8567c4db78` run. The third run, on the new driver, agrees on every figure; that is corroboration, not the measurement.
- **`tests/probe_red_demo.js`'s COUNT IS WITHHELD.** Four reads in this batch gave 15, 10, 9 and 8 COULD NOT BE APPLIED on an engine tree that did not move between the last three (`engine_release.js drift('f0f10cd06861')` is `[]` before and after all of them). The file was being re-aimed by another agent while it was read — its diff carries `RE-AIMED 2026-09-08, MEASURE` headers on three of the five that changed answer — so every one of those reads is torn, **including the first, which happened to match the previous batch's number**. A caption is not a quarantine, so no count is stated. 2 HOLLOW is stable across all four, and the one hard failure (`WIRE 120 Parting Shot … reverted-arm=true`) is present in the previous batch's logs and in this batch's first and last reads, unchanged and inherited.
- **Basis.** unchanged. Same question, same pins, same sample; a reader can be told ordering 16 became 14.
- **Supersedes.** ~~`ordering` stands at **16 games** and narration at **56 raw / 55 by the gate clause**~~ retracted as a current statement — they described release `fb0058fb5702` and the current values are 14 and 54 / 53. Both stood only in `docs/ENGINE.md`'s batch-2 section, which is a dated batch record and keeps them as what it measured. No living document outside this page states either. No quarantined figure is read or quoted; R1, R2, R3 and R4 stay withheld.
- **Owed to the next major.** Nothing published moves — the quarantine is still shut and this row claims nothing downstream of it. **Eight ordering mechanisms are named, diagnosed and unclosed** in `docs/ENGINE.md`'s batch-P hand list, two of which (the Substitute's step-0 interception and the single speed-sorted `DamagingHit` event) need a bisect budget because they reorder dice draws at an `nth`-counted address, and two of which (a turn-order pair and a post-KO replacement pair) are worth the most beyond narration because a turn-order rule reaches boards everywhere. **Named and not fixed, for the divisions that own them:** `engine/status.js` compares an artifact's stamped release against the POINTER in `data/engine-release.json` rather than against the live tree, so an engine edit made after a measurement left the gate reading **8 of 9 PASS on bytes that had stopped existing** — `engine_release.js drift` said so and the gate did not; that is MEASURE's. And an agent editing `engine/game_differential.js`, `engine/steering.js` and `tests/probe_red_demo.js` in the live tree while another measures against them is the *"a measuring agent may not run beside a writing agent"* rule arriving from the reader's side. Full account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-08-narration-ordering-2.md`.

## [5.267.0] — 2026-09-08 — a MILTANK decision is ~90% simulator and 0% MAG, and the scarce thing is menu coverage rather than rollouts
- **What changed.** No source file. A profile, written to an artifact: `profile_decision.js` (scratchpad) opens release `cf8567c4db78`, serves the simulator and its data from the snapshot, compiles `board.js` and `rollout_leaf.js` from the snapshot's bytes under their LIVE filenames, installs MILTANK on a `magnemite` player inside a real `BattleStream` game at the SHIPPED `MT.DEFAULTS`, and brackets a V8 CPU profile around ONE decision at a time. Two independent instruments — the sampling profile and nesting-guarded `hrtime` wrappers on the engine seam — plus a `NO_SEAM=1` control that removes the wrappers entirely. Answers Will's question of 2026-09-08 about whether engine optimisation is worth doing, before anything is optimised.
- **Measured.** WITHHELD, 2026-09-09 — every figure this bullet carried was read out of `data/search-decision-profile.json`, which `engine/quarantine.js` withholds (the profile drives the shipped MILTANK through MEDICHAM), and `tests/test-docs-quarantine.js` refused the row; the numbers came out rather than being captioned, per CLAUDE.md. What stands without a number: of one real in-game decision's self time, instrument excluded, the simulator and the tag layer together are nearly all of it, with `rollout_leaf.js`, `board.js`, `miltank.js` and `position_features.js` behind them and `magnemite.js` (MAG) at 0%; the leaf is nearly the whole decision inclusive (`leafAfterActions`), and the independent wall-clock seam agrees that `MEDI.battleTurn` dominates with `MEDI.battleInit` negligible. Per decision the search runs a few hundred to a few thousand playouts in a handful of leaf calls at a mean playout length well under the census-derived horizon, and most of a playout's cost is per-turn rather than fixed setup, so truncating the horizon is worth a real multiple. Structural waste, found and not fixed: `rollout_leaf.applySideState` rescans the move tag table four times per playout, and `tags.norm`, an unmemoised regex replace, is the single largest tag-layer cost with `param`, `tagsFor` and `withTag` behind it. Re-run once the gate opens — `profile_decision.js` on the current release — and restate the shares here from the artifact.
