- **Basis.** unchanged. New question; no series moved.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing. No quarantined figure is read or quoted; R1, R2, R3 and R4 stay withheld.
- **Owed to the next major.** Nothing published moves. **Three defects filed and deliberately not fixed:** (1) **a frozen release cannot serve `rollout_leaf.census()`** — `data/rollout-switch-census.json` is not in `engine_release.js SOURCES`, so a run that loads `rollout_leaf.js` from a snapshot path silently plays a switchless playout at horizon 60 instead of the shipped rate at horizon 14, prints one stderr line and reports success; MEASURE's, and the same shape as the `pp.js`/`mc_key.js` growths except that it does not throw. (2) **`miltank.js` seeds `evalPair` from `Date.now()`**, so a decision cannot be replayed — two runs on the same release, teams and battle seed diverged at move 1; SEARCH's, and a fix changes what is clicked so it needs its own arm. (3) **`budgetMs` overran** — all four consecutive decisions in the profile ran well past the configured budget (the readings are in the withheld artifact), because one finalist at n=400 runs to completion past the checkpoint; against a 55 s per-turn cap that is a live clock hazard. Also **named and not fixed:** the flag table in `docs/MILTANK.md` prints `--rollout-turns 60`, which is the fallback — the shipped default is the census-derived 14, and the run printed it. Full account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-08-decision-profile.md`.

## [5.267.0] — 2026-09-08 — the dead ratio came OUT of six living documents, and the npm oracle's legal sets are identical to ours

- **What changed.** No engine byte. (1) **Six living documents stated a superseded speed figure as the CURRENT one and have been REWRITTEN, not captioned** — the set was DERIVED from `engine/docs_scan.js` `livingDocs()` (25 documents) rather than taken from the brief's count of three, and the derivation found three more than a `24.9` search does: `docs/ABRA-deck-plain-english.md` slide 9f said *"about 25 times slower"*, `docs/MEW-whitepaper.md` §4.1 said *"117× faster"*, and `docs/GAME-DIFFERENTIAL-DESIGN.md` §7 said MEDICHAM *"measures ~1,600–2,000 battles/sec"*. The other three are `docs/ABRA-whitepaper.md` (§3.0 and open-question 8), `docs/ABRA-technical-docs.md` (the 3.62.2 correction and the *"do not quote an engine-speed figure"* rule — which also claimed no script measures the two engines, and two now do), `docs/SUMMARY.md`, and `docs/MODELS.md` under CHAMPIONS_SIM. `docs/ADR-002-showdown-is-the-authority.md` is a living document AND a dated decision record, so its 2026-08-06 block is kept, marked superseded, and given a 2026-09-08 block beside it. (2) A new read-only harness, `data/verification/npm-oracle-2026-09-08/compare_oracle_legality.js`, which asks whether the published npm Showdown build changes what is legal in this format.
- **Measured.** The ratio is **3.94x**: MEDICHAM **1,482 turns/sec / 134 whole games/sec / 0.675 ms/turn** against Showdown's raw `Battle` at **375 / 33.7 / 2.665**, 12,000 games per engine, 8 contention-free reps spanning 3.75–4.07, noise floor **0.3% / 2.7%** by half-split of one arm, both arms reaching a real result in 100% of games at 11.05 against 11.13 mean turns. Against `BattleStream` plus the official `RandomPlayerAI` it is **16.1x** at 92 turns/sec. Pins: release `fb0058fb5702`, `--team-store data/team-pool-frozen`, Showdown commit `20ad99ff`, census digest `b599f8d581b5`, flags `--pairs 40 --per-pair 10 --reps 6|9 --caps 60,14`. Artifacts `data/verification/speed-2026-09-08/`; the measurement itself is the row below and is not re-measured here. **The npm oracle:** `LEGAL_SETS_IDENTICAL: true` — 347 species, 500 moves, 148 items, 316 abilities with **zero** members on either side only and **zero `isNonstandard` differences**, an identical 29-rule resolved rule table, identical definitions for all three Champions formats, **14,192 learnset cells with 0 diffs**, and `TeamValidator` on every open-sheet team in the frozen pool with **0 accept/reject differences** (6,450 valid on both). Artifact `data/verification/npm-oracle-2026-09-08/npm-oracle-legality.json`, digest manifest beside it: **6 of 323 files differ** under `dist/data` and `dist/sim`, and every one is accounted for.
- **TWO FIGURES WERE WITHDRAWN MID-PASS RATHER THAN PUBLISHED, AND BOTH CAME FROM THE REPORT THIS PASS WAS FOLDING IN.** (1) ~~MEDICHAM's throughput fell **8.8x** in 33 days~~ retracted — the bisection Will asked for opened by killing its own premise, and the documents state the reason instead of a number: **13,041 / 217 is 60.1 turns per battle**, every battle running to the 60-turn cap on an engine that did not finish games; a same-day sibling reading is **2.0**; **1,482 / 134 is 11.06** with 100% of games reaching a result. Three populations of "a turn", so no slowdown figure is published in any living document. `engine/game_differential.js` also refuses every release before 2026-08-12, so the window both disputed figures came from cannot be re-measured by the instrument that produced today's. (2) ~~MILTANK's per-decision budget buys **~15 leaf calls per decision against Showdown's ~4**~~ retracted — it counted leaf CALLS as though they were playouts, when a call at `n=200` is 200 playouts. It stood in this pass's drafts of the white paper, `SUMMARY.md`, ADR-002, the deck and `docs/MEASURE.md`, and is DELETED from all five. **Neither replacement quotes the profile that refuted it**, because `data/search-decision-profile.json` is withheld. Accounts: `docs/_reports/2026-09-08-speed-regression-bisect.md`.
- **A QUARANTINE GATE WAS RED ON `docs/MEASURE.md` AND WAS FIXED IN THIS PASS, NOT FILED.** `tests/test-docs-quarantine.js` failed at HEAD: the 2026-09-08 speed section quoted a leaf-cost range out of `engine/bench_speed.js`'s consolidated artifact, which is one of the **63 artifacts `engine/quarantine.js` withholds**. Three figures came out of that paragraph rather than gaining a caveat, and the same treatment was applied everywhere else the intermediate 2026-08-28 throughput reading had been written: it is **named as withheld and not stated** in any living document. The gate reads **all clauses PASS** on every document this pass touched. The prior row below still carries that midpoint; it is a log entry and is not edited to agree with today.
- **Basis.** unchanged. Same question ADR-001 and ADR-002 asked, re-measured on the same machine; a reader can be told 24.9x became 3.94x, which is a routine revision of a series and not a change of basis.
- **Supersedes.** ~~the speed margin over Showdown is **24.9x**~~ retracted **as a current statement** — it stood as the CURRENT ratio in six living documents and has been DELETED from all six, replaced by 3.94x. The figure itself remains a correct measurement of the engine of 2026-08-06 and is not struck from the dated records that report it as such: `docs/ADR-001-use-the-champions-mod.md` and `docs/ROADMAP.md` carry it, are **not in the derived living set**, and are deliberately untouched. **This row overrides the judgement in the row below** that ADR-002 should also be left alone: ADR-002 IS in the living set, and its correction block did not merely record 24.9x, it built the load-bearing argument *"the gap is large enough to sit under a per-turn rollout search"* on it. That argument does not survive 3.94x and is retired in the new block rather than left standing. Also ~~*"there is no script in this repository that measures the speed of the two engines"*~~ retracted — `engine/bench_speed.js` and `data/verification/speed-2026-09-08/bench_two_engines.js` both do; it stood in `docs/ABRA-technical-docs.md` and has been corrected there.
- **Owed to the next major.** Nothing from this row: the rewrite IS the fold-in, done in this pass rather than deferred. **Named and not fixed, for the divisions that own them:** the *"requires a built master checkout"* header in `engine/champions_sim.js` and the same claim in both ADRs are ENGINE's, and **MEASURE recommends the npm switch and did not perform it** — an immutable published version instead of a git checkout pinned by convention, on evidence that the legal sets are identical. `docs/ROADMAP.md` §5.3 still narrates 24.9x as the correction to #61 and is outside this pass's write scope. **MEDICHAM's turns/sec still is not on the status board** beside its mechanics count, and the 8.8x lost in 33 days is the argument for putting it there. The CAUSE of the slowdown is **open** — a bisection is running and no document names one. Full account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-08-retract-24x-and-npm-legality.md`.

## [5.267.0] — 2026-09-08 — MEDICHAM is 3.94x faster than Showdown's own simulator, not 117x and not 24.9x, and the npm package has shipped the Champions mod since July

- **What changed.** Nothing in the engine. A measurement: `data/verification/speed-2026-09-08/bench_two_engines.js` plays MEDICHAM and Showdown's `Battle` **interleaved in one process**, on the same pinned team pairs, under the same `runPlayout(explore=1.0, foePolicy='uniform', switchRate=0.0998)` policy, and reports the **paired per-rep ratio** so machine contention is common-mode. `bench_showdown_stream.js` adds the arm a public user would actually write — `BattleStream` + the official `RandomPlayerAI`. Answers Will's question of 2026-09-08 about whether a simulator like MEDICHAM already exists and whether the speed rationale holds.
- **Measured.** MEDICHAM **134 whole games/sec, 1,482 turns/sec (0.675 ms/turn)** against Showdown's **33.7 games/sec, 375 turns/sec (2.665 ms/turn)** — a ratio of **3.94x** (8 contention-free reps, min 3.75 / max 4.07 at cap 60; median 4.00, 3.85–4.18 at cap 14). **Noise floor 0.3% / 2.7%** by half-split of the same arm on ms/turn, so the effect is ~100x the floor. Sample **12,000 games per engine**; both arms reached a real result in **100%** of cap-60 games at **11.05 vs 11.13** mean turns. Against `BattleStream` + `RandomPlayerAI` the gap is **16.1x** (8.62 games/sec, 92 turns/sec, fastest of 3 x 100 games). Artifacts: `data/verification/speed-2026-09-08/two-engine-speed-{main,run2,npm}.json` and `showdown-stream-run.log`. Pins: release **`fb0058fb5702`**, `--team-store data/team-pool-frozen`, Showdown commit `20ad99ffc9a5a4a4e8fb56ab04ad8e4255b3f2b4`, census digest `b599f8d581b5`, flags `--pairs 40 --per-pair 10 --reps 6|9 --caps 60,14`. `engine/game_differential.js` digest `9c61584c1e4a` recorded before and after every run and unchanged, so a live ENGINE agent's edits did not reach it. **THROUGHPUT ONLY — no accuracy claim; MEDICHAM stays quarantined for correctness.**
- **Basis.** unchanged. Same question ADR-001 and ADR-002 asked, on the same machine, re-measured; a reader can be told 24.9x became 3.94x.
- **Supersedes.** The ratio, as a **routine revision of a series, not a retraction**. `24.9x` (2026-08-06) was a correct measurement of the engine of that date and stays valid as one; **3.94x** is the current value and is what a reader must be told now. The earlier throughput figures are left standing and are NOT struck out, deliberately: the white paper already frames them as *"three measurements of MEDICHAM's throughput"*, ADR-001 and ADR-002 are dated decision records whose evidence may not be rewritten in place, and striking a series member would fire the retraction registry against six documents that are each correctly reporting what was true when they were written. **What IS retracted, because it is a statement of fact that is now false:** ~~the Champions mod is not in the published npm package, so a built master checkout is required~~ retracted — it was true of the version the claim names and is false of the current one, which defines `[Gen 9 Champions] VGC 2026 Reg M-B`, loads as mod `champions` gametype `doubles`, is **byte-identical to our pinned checkout in 7 of the 8 mod files**, and benchmarks at **2.588 ms/turn against the checkout's 2.579**. It stands in `engine/champions_sim.js`'s header and in both ADRs.
- **Owed to the next major.** **Three living documents state `24.9x` as the CURRENT ratio and it is not** — `docs/ABRA-whitepaper.md` (§0.3 and the ADR-002 correction block), `docs/ABRA-technical-docs.md` (two places) and `docs/SUMMARY.md`. That sentence must be REWRITTEN to 3.94x in the document pass, not captioned; it is the one thing on this row that actively misleads a reader today. `docs/ADR-001-*.md` and `docs/ADR-002-*.md` state it too and are **deliberately left alone** — a decision record is dated evidence and is not edited in place. The MEDICHAM throughput series in the white paper gains a fourth entry (**1,482 turns/sec, 2026-09-08, release `fb0058fb5702`**) rather than losing its first three. **Named and not fixed, for the divisions that own them:** the npm-package claim in `engine/champions_sim.js`'s header is ENGINE's; whether a 4x margin is worth an engine is a SEARCH question with a decisive-pair answer (ROADMAP #62) and is NOT settled here. **MEDICHAM's turns/sec belongs on the status board beside its mechanics count** — 13,041 → ~1,800 (`data/medicham-speed.json`, 2026-08-28) → 1,482 in 33 days is the two numbers trading against each other, and only one of them is printed today. Full account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-08-engine-speed-comparison.md`.

## [5.267.0] — 2026-09-07 — the narration gate's ordering class: two immunity families at the wrong hit step, an arriving ability speaking between two switches, and a pivot that would not name its refuser
- **What changed.** `engine/medicham2-browser.js`, three fixes, each with its own new probe and its own `MEDI_*` restore knob, each shown RED first. (1) **Two immunity families answered at the wrong step of `trySpreadMoveHit`, in opposite directions.** The walk is STEP-MAJOR (`sim/battle-actions.ts:550-610`), so the step decides the ORDER of the `-immune` lines on a spread hit and the slot order does not: **Levitate/Eelevate** have NO handler at all upstream (`data/abilities.ts:2301` is a comment pointing at `Pokemon#isGrounded`) and are announced inside `runImmunity` at **step 2**, while this engine answered them from `absorbedBy` at **step 1**; **Soundproof/Bulletproof/Overcoat** are plain `onTryHit` (`:4426`, `:470`, `:3098`) at **step 1**, while this engine asked `moveClassBlocked` at **step 3**. `isGrounded` gained an optional `why` out-param so the ATTRIBUTION is asked of its own clause order rather than of the ability name — Gravity, Ingrain, Smack Down and Iron Ball answer above it and the FLYING clause answers above it too. (2) **An arriving ability announced between two `|switch|` lines.** `switchIn` writes the line and QUEUES `{choice:'runSwitch'}` (`sim/battle-actions.ts:145-158`); `runSwitch` drains every consecutive one into ONE speed-sorted `fieldEvent('SwitchIn', switchersIn)` (`:175-186`); an ability's `onStart` runs AS an `onSwitchIn` handler inside it (`Battle#getCallback`, `sim/battle.ts:1018-1031`). Supreme Overlord's `-activate`/`-start` is now collected as a thunk on the deferred entrant and fired inside `runEntryPass`. **The Magic Room item park was deliberately NOT moved** — it is a state write, not an announcement, and no card lands on it. (3) **The pivot branch printed a bare `|-immune|` for a move-class refusal**, which is the half WIRE 241 left behind in the same `if` when it split Good as Gold out of it; Parting Shot is the only pivot in this format carrying the `sound` flag.
- **Measured.** Whole-game **NARRATION-ONLY 63 → 55 of 961** (raw 64 → 56 before the declared row is subtracted), **protocol-parted 67 → 59**, **causes 65 → 57**, and the `ordering` class **24 → 16 games** — `data/game-differential.json`, release `fb0058fb5702`, `--steering empirical --arm middle --end-state --state --census data/verification/census-pin-9446a684709d.json --games 1200 --turns 50 --team-store data/team-pool-frozen`, **961 played / 958 readable / 3 void**, against the same pins on baseline release `f30bf025ae28` (`data/verification/batchO/gd-baseline.json`). **BOARD-MATERIAL is 0 of 958 in every one of the four runs** — `state.games` less `state.games_board_never_diverged`, which is the gate's clause and not `by_cause_totals.games_board_material`. Three predictions were written before their runs (`data/verification/_prediction-2026-09-07-batchO-{immune-step,immune-step2,entry-and-pivot}.json`); the last two **hit at the point estimate on every clause** and the first missed on the net, for the reason in the next bullet. Roster re-run on the new release and flat: **items 140 of 148, abilities 146 of 202, moves 487 of 500, 0 FIRED-AND-BOARDS-DIFFER, 0 DID-NOT-FIRE, 0 `NOT CAUGHT`, 94 red demonstrations 0 not-ok** — `data/roster.{items,abilities,moves}.json`. Census regenerated and **unmoved at 830 live / 830 probed / 0 missing** — `data/mechanics-census.json`; the only content change is one Monte-Carlo detail string.
- **THE FIRST CUT CLOSED FIVE GAMES AND OPENED THREE, AND THE RUN IS WHAT SAID SO.** On release `fa835f7a4939` all five `-immune` ordering causes closed and three games re-entered as `-immune field 3`. **Two were the fix's own fault**: `runImmunity`'s ternary reaches `isGrounded` only when the move type is **Ground** (`sim/pokemon.ts:2270`), so a Psycho Cut into a Hydreigon — a DARK immunity — was announced `[from] ability: Levitate`. The correction then exposed a second fault one question over: the step-1 deferral was asking the ATTRIBUTION reader where the question is MEMBERSHIP, so a **Rotom-Fan** (Electric/**Flying** *and* Levitate) fell back into step 1 and was attributed; that arm had been written as a CONTROL and the `--red` run proved it a **third red the pre-batch engine also failed**, with no pool witness because Rotom-Fan is not in the frozen team store. **The third transfer was not the fix's fault at all** — Parting Shot into Soundproof was pre-existing and invisible until the ordering divergence two lines earlier in the same turn was closed, which is the worked example of why a divergence count is a LOWER BOUND. Figures from `fa835f7a4939` are recorded as a withdrawn step and are not carried forward.
- **Basis.** unchanged. Same gate, same clauses, same pins, same sample; one figure moved and the story did not.
- **Supersedes.** ~~whole-game NARRATION-ONLY **63 of 961** across **62 causes**~~ retracted — superseded by **55 of 961 across 57 causes** on release `fb0058fb5702`. It stood only in `docs/ENGINE.md`'s previous hand list, which is a dated log entry and is not edited. No living document states it. The gate reading moves **8 of 9 → 6 of 9 clauses PASS**, and that is not a regression: moving the engine restamped five artifacts as `MEASURED AGAINST A DIFFERENT ENGINE`, and **all five were re-run in this pass on `fb0058fb5702` and are green** — `data/engine-diff.json` (0 of 6000 at the midpoint and both corners), the three `data/roster.*.json`, and `data/all-mechanics-fire.json`. `node engine/status.js` reads **8 of 9 clauses PASS**, unchanged from before this batch, and the sole failure is this batch's own clause at its new value.
- **Owed to the next major.** `docs/ENGINE.md` (this pass's hand list, already written) and the narration figure in `docs/ABRA-whitepaper.md` and `docs/SUMMARY.md`. Named and not fixed: the `ordering` class still holds **16 games** — a residual paid on the wrong body first (3), a `faint` against an end-of-turn line (3), Substitute against a damage line (3), and seven single-card mechanisms; the **Zero to Hero `-activate` moved with Supreme Overlord and nothing stages it**; the **Magic Room item park** is still written at the placement; two `onTryHit` refusers on one spread hit are not modelled (`Battle.compareLeftToRightOrder`, `sim/battle.ts:421`); and **`tests/probe_red_demo.js` is still red at HEAD**, measured rather than assumed at **15 COULD NOT BE APPLIED on the way in, 18 after this batch's engine changes, and 15 again after the three it broke were re-aimed** (WIRE 126 and both WIRE 128 entries, all three patching the type-immunity gate or `moveClassBlocked`; the Bulletproof one had to MOVE HOUSE, because the line its pattern named is now `MEDI_IMMUNE_STEP_LEGACY`'s arm and a patch aimed at it would have modified a branch the demonstration never enters). The inherited 15 and 2 HOLLOW are untouched. Full account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-07-narration-ordering.md`.

## [5.267.0] — 2026-09-07 — the green backed by nothing is now backed by something, and Focus Sash agrees with Showdown for the first time
- **What changed.** `tests/roster.js` only — **no engine byte moved** (`git diff --stat engine/` empty; `data/engine-release.json` still points at `f30bf025ae28`). Two faults, either of which alone would have hidden `item/hp-floor`'s plant. (1) `INTERFERES` gained **`onPrepareHit`**, so a derived carrier may not rewrite itself as the move leaves: `carrierAbility()` had given Meowscarada its **Protean**, the body clicked the roster's own control click (Focus Energy), Protean rewrote it `dark/grass → normal` **on the board**, and the X-Scissor `lethalMove()` had sized at 4x against the DEX typing landed neutral for **69 of 151** — so no body reached the HP floor, the Sash never fired, and dropping the floor from the simulator moved nothing. (2) `controlOf()` now also ignores the **party-row copy** of the subject's held item: `board_state.js` writes the item twice and only the active slot was ignored, so *"Showdown's board moved when the item was added"* was **true of every item row by construction** and the inert gate could not fire on any of them. The Sash row's entire `sd_delta` was four leaves, all `p2.party.meowscarada.item` — the control arm describing itself. **The plant itself is byte-identical to what it was; it was not weakened or re-aimed.**
- **Measured.** Roster **items 140 of 148, abilities 146 of 202, moves 487 of 500, 0 FIRED-AND-BOARDS-DIFFER and 0 DID-NOT-FIRE on all three** — `data/roster.{items,abilities,moves}.json`, each re-run `--reds --write --release f30bf025ae28`, arm `top-tie-first`, format `gen9championsvgc2026regmb`. `data/roster.items.json` now carries `plant_anchors.reds_ran: true`, **18 red rows, 0 not ok** (was `reds[9] {"ok":false,"moved":null}`), and **zero `NOT CAUGHT` across all three stages** — items 18/18, abilities 40/40, moves 36/36. The failing probe and its receipt: `tests/probe_reds_plant_reaches.js --release f30bf025ae28 --stage items --rule item/hp-floor`, **`NO REACH … 0 leaf/leaves` → `REACHES … 29 leaf/leaves`** (`t2 p2.party.meowscarada.hp clean=1 planted=0`). The new filter was printed before it was wired: exactly **three** legal abilities in this format register `onPrepareHit` — Protean, Libero, Parental Bond, all three disqualifying, no over-match — and it moves **two carriers and no candidate**. The second fix was measured before it was added: **0 of 140** greens rested only on that leaf and **140 of 140** move a leaf the item actually caused, so it plugs the hole and moves no row.
- **BOARD-MATERIAL 0 of 958 is UNTOUCHED, not re-measured, and the distinction is structural rather than assumed** — `data/game-differential.json` on release `f30bf025ae28`, `--games 1200 --turns 50 --arm middle --steering empirical --end-state --census data/verification/census-pin-9446a684709d.json --team-store data/team-pool-frozen`, 961 played / 958 readable / 3 void. `engine/game_differential.js` reads the simulator out of the frozen release and does not read `tests/roster.js` at all, and no engine byte moved. **The census was deliberately NOT regenerated**: no probe and no mechanic changed, so `tests/test-mechanics.js` would write 830 identical rows under a new digest — and that digest is the pin steering the board-material sample, so rewriting it would cost the pin's comparability and buy nothing. Census stays **830 live / 830 probed** — `data/mechanics-census.json`, untouched in `git status`.
- **FOCUS SASH AGREES WITH SHOWDOWN, AND THAT HAD NEVER BEEN MEASURED.** Champions does not override it — no `focussash` entry in `/data/mods/champions/items.ts`, and the format's resolved handler is byte-identical to `Dex.mod('gen9')`'s — so `pokemon-showdown/data/items.ts:2265` governs: full HP **and** lethal **and** from a Move. All three clauses now land and both engines are identical leaf for leaf: a full-HP Meowscarada survives at **1 HP with the item spent**; a Whimsicott one chip off full takes a lethal Dire Claw and **dies**; the survivor on 1 HP with the sash gone takes a second X-Scissor and **is not saved twice**. **No engine defect was found — the demonstration was blind and the engine was right.**
- **Basis.** unchanged. Same gate, same clauses, same pins; one clause moved from FAIL to PASS on a repaired instrument.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing published. The gate reading moves **7 of 9 → 8 of 9 clauses PASS** (`node engine/status.js`), and the sole remaining failure is whole-game **NARRATION, 63 of 961**, which is unchanged and not this pass's. The `7 of 9` figures standing in `docs/ABRA-whitepaper.md` all sit inside version-stamped historical blocks narrating what the gate read at 5.255.0–5.263.0; they are dated waypoints rather than claims about the live gate, and are not edited in place — the same treatment the `113` waypoint already has in `data/docs-currency-baseline.json`. The previous row's finding that `focussash`'s green was **a green backed by nothing** is answered rather than retracted; that row is a log entry and is not edited.
- **Owed to the next major.** `docs/ENGINE.md` (this pass's hand list, already written) and the gate reading in `docs/ABRA-whitepaper.md` and `docs/SUMMARY.md`. Four items are owed and named rather than fixed: `chipFor()` sizes the chip off `CAST.ATTACKER()` while the script throws it from `CAST.ATTACKER2()` (predicted 22 off Whimsicott, landed **11** — hides nothing today, but the rule's printed `note` states a number the game does not produce); `controlOf()` strips the item from the subject only, so `item/hp-floor`'s negative body holds a Focus Sash in both arms; and the inert-click assertion is proved on the four fixed cast bodies rather than on the DERIVED carriers, which is the general form of fault 1 and is unenumerated. Full account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-07-focus-sash-blind-test.md`.

## [5.267.0] — 2026-09-07 — the settled tree was re-measured, four clauses were passing all along, and one green in the items stage is backed by nothing
- **What changed.** No source file. This is a MEASUREMENT pass on a settled tree: release `f30bf025ae28` cut on the current bytes, then every clause the concurrent-engine-hold staled was re-run — the whole-game differential, all three roster stages, and the mechanics census. Three agents had held the engine at once earlier in the day, so `engine/status.js` was withholding six clauses as `MEASURED AGAINST A DIFFERENT ENGINE`. Nothing was known to be wrong; the picture was unmeasured, which this project has written down as the worse of the two states.
- **Measured.** The gate reads **7 of 9 clauses PASS, 2 FAIL, gate still CLOSED** — `node engine/status.js` on release `f30bf025ae28`. **BOARD-MATERIAL 0 of 958 games**, 10,675 of 10,675 turn boundaries identical, 0 games cut off by the turn cap — `data/game-differential.json`, `--steering empirical --arm middle --end-state --census data/verification/census-pin-9446a684709d.json --games 1200 --turns 50 (default) --team-store data/team-pool-frozen`, n = 1200 requested / 961 played / 958 readable / 3 void, pool digest `0d103fb9fa87`, census digest `9446a684709d`. **NARRATION-ONLY 63 of 961 = 6.6% across 62 causes** (64 raw, 1 declared) — same artifact, and 60 of the 62 causes occur in exactly one game. Roster **items 140 of 148, abilities 146 of 202, moves 487 of 500, with 0 FIRED-AND-BOARDS-DIFFER and 0 DID-NOT-FIRE on all three** — `data/roster.{items,abilities,moves}.json`. Census **830 live / 830 probed / 0 missing** — `data/mechanics-census.json`; and `data/all-mechanics-fire.json` is numerically identical to its superseded self (moves diverged 4, abilities 1, items 0), only `engine_release` moved.
- **Basis.** unchanged. Cap 50 is the current default and the previous cap-50 measurement is the series this joins; the cap-20 figures are the ones that may not be lined up against it, and that basis change was declared in the row below.
- **THE ITEMS CLAUSE FAILS ON ITS OWN SAFETY NET, NOT ON THE ENGINE.** `item/hp-floor`'s red demonstration applied its break and **nothing caught it** — `data/roster.items.json` `reds[9]`, `{"ok":false,"moved":null}`, with `plant_anchors {checked:18, dead:[], reds_ran:true}`, so the anchor matched exactly once and the patch landed. The rule's only member is `focussash`, whose `FIRED-AND-BOARDS-MATCH` is therefore currently **a green backed by nothing**. This is new evidence rather than a regression: the superseded artifact carries `reds_ran: false`, so its zero bad reds counted zero reds. Handed to ENGINE; MEASURE does not repair staging.
- **SEVEN OF THE 28 SURVIVING `COULD-NOT-STAGE` REASONS CARRY A CLAUSE THAT IS NO LONGER TRUE, AND TWO FALL ENTIRELY.** `leppaberry` and `shedshell` are EXPIRED outright — PP **is** a compared board leaf (`board_state.js:1503/1591`, red-demonstrated in this very run at `p1.pp[0].gunkshot`), and the harness **does** have a switch action (`switchVerdict`, `tests/roster.js:1711`), staged on ten rows today including Shadow Tag. `focusenergy` and `struggle` each lose a clause to the same two facts while a second clause still binds. `focusband`, `kingsrock` and `quickclaw` cite `game_differential.js PRIMARY_ARM`, which has meant the **`middle`** arm since 2026-08-13 and now says the opposite of the words quoted from it. `galewings` is CANNOT TELL and misdescribed — it names Drill Peck as Talonflame's *"own Flying click"* and Talonflame cannot learn it. `guarddog` has zero legal carriers and is misfiled as an in-scope gap. `imprison` and `memento` are not reasons at all: the harness threw.
- **THE DOCUMENTATION GATE WENT RED ON THE RE-MEASUREMENT AND WAS FIXED IN THIS PASS, NOT FILED.** `tests/test-docs-current.js` clause 3b(b) gained two entries. (a) `docs/ABRA-technical-docs.md:17` still stated ~~`data/roster.abilities.json` tests 129 of 202~~ and ~~`data/roster.moves.json` tests 475 of 500~~ — **retracted**, corrected in place to **146** and **487**. Those figures were superseded in writing by the roster row below, whose retraction landed in `docs/SUMMARY.md` and MISSED this document; the citation only passed until now because both numbers happened to occur elsewhere in the five co-cited artifacts. A retraction never waits for a major, so it lands here. (b) `docs/ABRA-whitepaper.md:59` cites `113` inside a block stamped `5.262.0` narrating a fix sequence (*"protocol 114 to 113"*) — a historical waypoint, never a claim about the live artifact, which passed only by coincidence until `data/game-differential.json` was republished. Editing a dated block in place is forbidden, so it is a hand-edited ratchet entry in `data/docs-currency-baseline.json` with its reason recorded beside it: **a reported defect, not an approval.** Gate re-run: **33 passed, 0 failed.**
- **Supersedes.** ~~`data/roster.abilities.json` tests 129 of 202~~ and ~~`data/roster.moves.json` tests 475 of 500~~ retracted — they stood in `docs/ABRA-technical-docs.md` and have been CORRECTED there in this pass. Nothing else: every other figure this pass replaces was being WITHHELD by the gate rather than published. The gate reading itself moves from 6 failing clauses to 2, and `web/quarantine-data.js` is now further drifted from the live gate than the bundle already declares.
- **Owed to the next major.** `docs/MEASURE.md` and `docs/ENGINE.md` (the gate reading), and the MEDICHAM gate figures in `docs/ABRA-whitepaper.md` and `docs/SUMMARY.md`. Full account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-07-settled-gate-and-reason-audit.md`.

## [5.267.0] — 2026-09-07 — the chance-gated abilities are staged, and the roster was pricing bodies the driver stopped building
- **What changed.** `tests/roster.js` and `tests/test-roster-arm-pin.js`. (1) A **LIVE-DIE LANE**: eight
  ability rules that DECLARE `arm: 'middle'` by name, each computing the address of the die its own
  mechanic turns on and choosing the click and the turn so that die comes up. `play()` reads both
  engines' address logs (`G.midAddresses()`) and `diceGate()` refuses any verdict whose COIN the two
  engines did not throw at the same address. **No other rule reaches the middle arm and the corner
  assertions in `tests/test-roster-arm-pin.js` §1–§3 are untouched**; §4 is new and requires every
  `middle` row to carry that receipt. (2) **`flatL50` now prices a body through `buildPair` + the
  engine's own `M.spreadL50`** instead of its own blank-spread arithmetic — the driver started putting
  a real Champions SP spread on every body and the roster's copy of the stat line did not follow, so
  every derived hit in the file was sized for a body that no longer exists. (3) `hitInBand` takes the
  SMALLEST in-band hit, `ability/base-power-scoped` no longer selects a CHARGE move, and
  `switchVerdict`'s refusal pattern was stale against the driver's message format.
- **Measured.** Release `028392265ab7`, all three stages. Abilities **129 → 146 FIRED-AND-BOARDS-MATCH,
  141 → 124 COULD-NOT-STAGE**; moves **475 → 487 / 22 → 10**; items **140 / 8, unchanged**. FIRED-AND-
  BOARDS-DIFFER and DID-NOT-FIRE are **0** on all three stages before and after. — `data/roster.abilities.json`,
  `data/roster.moves.json`, `data/roster.items.json`. Eleven `middle` rows, every one `coin_shared`,
  every one `sd_only: 0 / me_only: 0` over the whole game.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Supersedes.** The roster triple and the red-demonstration counts in the 5.208.0 block of
  `docs/SUMMARY.md` — **DELETED there in this pass, not captioned.** They were taken before this lane
  and before the stat predictor was corrected, and the three artifacts no longer contain them. What
  that block CLAIMED — zero FIRED-AND-BOARDS-DIFFER, zero DID-NOT-FIRE, every red demonstration
  caught, no dead anchors — is re-measured above and still stands.
- **Owed to the next major.** none.

## [6.0.0] — 2026-09-07 — the turn cap was the horizon, and the horizon was hiding a board divergence at turn 22
- **What changed.** `engine/game_differential.js` and `engine/medicham2-browser.js`. (1) **THE DEFAULT TURN CAP IS 50, NOT 20** (`TURNS_DEFAULT`). At `--turns 20` the board-material bar read `0 of 958` while **35 of 958 games were still running when the instrument stopped watching**, and one of them parted a board at **turn 22**. (2) **THE TRUNCATION IS PRINTED NOW** — `state.games_cut_off_by_the_turn_cap`, published in the artifact and printed directly under the bar with an explicit warning when non-zero; shown RED on a deliberate `--turns 12` run at 48 of 129. (3) **A PER-TURN-BOOST VOLATILE DIES WHEN ITS SOURCE FAINTS, not only when it vacates a slot.** `syrupbomb.condition.onUpdate` tests `!source.isActive` (`data/moves.ts:18770-18774`) and `faintMessages` clears `isActive` at the faint (`sim/battle.ts:2563`), four statements after the `|faint|` line and long before a replacement. This engine tested SLOT MEMBERSHIP under a comment asserting the opposite in as many words, so a Syrup Bomb whose Hydrapple was KILLED kept taking a Speed stage every residual from a corpse. One fact had two implementations — the partial-trap sweep already had it right — and both sites now call one `sourceOffField`. Probe `tests/probe_syrupbomb_source_faint.js`, knob `MEDI_VOLSRC_SLOT_ONLY`.
- **Measured.** Board-material **1 of 958 → 0 of 958 at `--turns 50`**, knob-controlled on ONE release — `data/verification/horizon/gd-cap50-{before,after}.json`, release `f30bf025ae28`, `--steering empirical --arm middle --end-state --state --census data/verification/census-pin-9446a684709d.json --games 1200 --turns 50 --team-store data/team-pool-frozen`, n = 961 played / 958 readable / 3 void. The cap sweep on identical pins: **cap 20 → 35 of 958 games ended AT THE CAP; cap 50 → 0; cap 100 → 0** (`gd-cap20.json`, `gd-cap50-after.json`, `gd-cap100.json`). The longest game in this pool reaches **turn 36**, so cap 50 and cap 100 play a byte-identical set of games: the two artifacts differ in exactly one key, `state.agreement_by_turn`, whose extra fifty rows all read `reached: 0`. Census unmoved at **830 live / 830 probed / 0 missing** — `data/mechanics-census.json`, regenerated on the fixed bytes.
- **Basis.** **CHANGED — a reader can no longer be told that a board-material figure covers whole games.** Every such figure published before today was measured at cap 12 or cap 20; at 20, thirty-five of 961 games were cut short and the figure was a claim about their first twenty turns. Cap 50 and cap 100 ARE one series (shown key by key above); cap 50 and cap 20 are NOT. This row is under `[Unreleased]` and clause 5d cannot judge it yet — **whoever cuts the version owes it `X.0.0` with the document set folded in**, or must show the samples identical.
- **The 1-versus-4 discrepancy is settled, and the BAR is right.** The bar is `state.games` less `state.games_board_never_diverged`, computed over `results` = `allResults.filter(r => !r._mid_void)` (`game_differential.js:7861-7868`). `by_cause_totals.games_board_material` sums `byCause`, built from the games whose PROTOCOL parted, and `endStateSummary` is called on the arm's **UNFILTERED** results (`:7509`). The gap is exactly the void-excluded games that part a board, and it is proven by control rather than read: with `--state-count-void`, same release and pins, the bar reads **3 of 961** and by-cause reads **3** — they coincide the moment the filter is removed (`gd-cap50-void.json`). A void game's dice streams are not shared, so its board split is the RULER and not the engine. A second, opposite gap exists — a board that parts with a silent narration would be in the bar and absent from the table — and both runs report it at **0** today.
- **Owed, and named.** (a) A **NARRATION-POSITION** row, handed to the NARRATION clause: the fixed game did not leave the run, it changed bucket (`BOARD-MATERIAL` → `NARRATION-ONLY`, new cause `ordering :: |faint|p2b <> |-end|p1a|syrupbomb`). medicham2 emits the `-end` above the `|faint|`; the authority emits the faint first (`battle-actions.ts:976` then `:1003`). (b) `!source.activeTurns`, the third clause of the authority's partial-trap predicate, is deliberately NOT in `sourceOffField` — it has no counterpart in `syrupbomb.condition` and stays declared at its own site. (c) `data/game-differential.json` is owed a republish on a settled tree at the new default; it ran on `bc99dcc268ce` and the tree is `f30bf025ae28`, so `engine/status.js` reads the board clause as **`MEASURED AGAINST A DIFFERENT ENGINE`** — **stale, not green, and not published over**, because the NARRATION agent owns that artifact tonight.
- **Red first, and my own probe was wrong twice before the engine was.** RED-1 shown red on the live tree before a byte moved (`showdown 1 Speed stage / medicham 2`, board parting on `active[].boosts.spe` and `active[].vol.syrupbomb` — the same leaf as the pool game), with CTRL-A (source PIVOTS out) and CTRL-B (source STAYS) holding in both arms. The two faults were mine: a faint REPLACEMENT is also a `|switch|`, so the first version called RED-1 mislabelled; and the disagreement counter was asserted `=== 1` when under the restore knob the same board is met on every Update until the replacement arrives (measured 5). `MEDSEEN.perTurnVolatileSourceFaintedInSlot` is asserted `> 0` in RED-1 and exactly `0` in both controls, so the knob is proven to reach the rule.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing retracted. Board-material ~~0 of 958 at `--turns 20`~~ is **not** retracted — it was a correct answer to a narrower question — but it may not be lined up against the cap-50 figure without the sample-identity check above.
- **Owed to the next major.** `docs/ENGINE.md`, `docs/MEASURE.md`, and the MEDICHAM gate figures in `docs/ABRA-whitepaper.md` and `docs/SUMMARY.md` — every one of them quotes a board-material figure measured at a horizon that has now moved. Full account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-07-horizon-and-hidden-divergence.md`.

## [5.267.0] — 2026-09-07 — narration batch 1: the second gate moved 72 → 63, the gate's own selftest was red because the first gate succeeded, and one fix parted two boards before it was right
- **What changed.** `engine/medicham2-browser.js` (three fixes), `engine/quarantine.js` (one selftest arm), `engine/game_differential.js` (three console counters, no artifact field). (1) **A ONE-ARRIVAL PARENTAL BOND VOLLEY ANNOUNCES NO `|-hitcount|`** — the authority's clause has a third term this engine never carried, `!(move.hit === 1 && move.multihitType === 'parentalbond')` (`data/mods/champions/scripts.ts:547-551`), and `multihitType` is written by Parental Bond alone, so every other multi-hit family that lands one arrival still prints `1`. (2) **MEGA SOL ANNOUNCES ITS PRIVATE SUN** on the five move handlers that pass `message` to `effectiveWeather` (`sim/pokemon.ts:2195-2202`) — Solar Beam, Solar Blade, Moonlight, Morning Sun, Synthesis. The membership is a tag SHAPE and `data/tags.json` was NOT regenerated; `tests/probe_megasol_announce.js` derives it from the tags AND from the live dex handlers on every run and fails on a difference of one entry either way. (3) **A VOLLEY DRAINS ONCE PER ARRIVAL** — `sim/battle.ts:2160-2171` sits inside `spreadDamage`, which `spreadMoveHit` runs once per hit. This was on batch N's owed list; fix 1 made it measurable by uncovering it in two games.
- **Measured.** Narration-only games **72 → 63 raw**, declared-adjusted **71 → 62**; protocol diverging games **75 → 66**; narration causes **70 → 61** — `data/game-differential.json`, release `bc99dcc268ce`, `--steering empirical --arm middle --end-state --state --census data/verification/census-pin-9446a684709d.json --games 1200 --turns 20 --team-store data/team-pool-frozen`, n = 961 played / 958 readable / 3 void, against the same flags and pins on release `1be57a100d59`. **BOARD-MATERIAL stays 0 of 958** with `state.first_board_divergences` empty. Census unmoved at **830 live / 830 probed / 0 missing** — `data/mechanics-census.json`. Eleven causes closed, two games transferred and then closed.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **THE FIRST CUT OF FIX 3 PARTED TWO BOARDS AND THE FIGURES FROM IT ARE NOT PUBLISHED.** Release `028392265ab7` passed the arrival's PACKET to the drain where the authority passes `targetDamage`, the HP the bar actually gave up, so an overkill healed on damage that never landed: **BOARD-MATERIAL 0 → 2**, a crit Drain Punch into a Toxapex on 2 HP healing 13 where the authority healed 1. That was the FIRST named risk written into `data/verification/_prediction-2026-09-07-narr1-drain.json` before the run, and the board clause is what caught it. The prediction file records the miss rather than being rewritten; the run's narration figures are withheld because it measured an engine this division does not ship.
- **Red first, and the probes were wrong three times before the engine was.** `tests/probe_bond_one_arrival_hitcount.js` (`MEDI_BOND_ONE_ARRIVAL_HITCOUNT=1`), `tests/probe_megasol_announce.js` (`MEDI_MEGASOL_SILENT=1`), `tests/probe_drain_per_arrival.js` (`MEDI_DRAIN_AT_FOOT=1`) — each shown RED under its own restore knob, each with knob-cleared controls that must hold in both arms. The faults: both p2 slots clicked Protect so four arms read two empty lists; an arm expected `-hitcount 1` where the authority wrote `2` and reading element `[0]` compared two identical 2s; and a drain arm labelled "arrival 1 kills" does not kill on arrival 1. All three corrected before any engine byte moved.
- **The gate's own selftest was RED and it is fixed.** `engine/quarantine.js --selftest` read 232 passed / 1 failed — *"the live gate carries exactly one reporting clause"*, `got []` — because `narrationClause` computes `gates` from the BOARD-MATERIAL clause's own verdict, so narration flipped from REPORTING to GATING the moment boards hit zero. The arm was a photograph of one side of a knob that was always going to turn. It now asserts the relation instead: exactly one narration clause, declaring `gates` as an explicit boolean, the only clause allowed to opt out, and gating *exactly when* the live board clause passes with a receipt. Shown red on a deliberate break first. **235 passed / 0 failed.**
- **Gate.** WITHHELD, and the reason is an incident rather than a result — see below. On release `bc99dcc268ce` the two whole-game clauses read BOARD-MATERIAL 0 of 958 and NARRATION 62 of 961; `node engine/status.js` cannot currently print either, because the live tree moved.
- **ANOTHER AGENT WROTE TO `engine/medicham2-browser.js` MID-BATCH, AT 19:28 LOCAL.** A `sourceOffField` / `MEDI_VOLSRC_SLOT_ONLY` change, plus `tests/probe_syrupbomb_source_faint.js` and a `tests/roster.js` edit, appeared while this batch ran. **Every figure in this row survives and that was checked rather than assumed**: each measurement opened a frozen release and `perTurnVolatileSourceFaintedInSlot` reads 0 in the frozen `engine/medicham2-browser.js` of all five releases used here and 2 in the live tree. What does not survive is the LIVE gate: `status.js` withholds both whole-game clauses as *"MEASURED AGAINST A DIFFERENT ENGINE — data/game-differential.json ran on release `bc99dcc268ce` and the tree is `f30bf025ae28`"* and prints 6 of 9 failing where this batch left 1 of 9. **The re-run is not this session's to make** — a differential on the combined tree measures both changes at once. `data/engine-diff.json` (re-run 19:36, stamped `f30bf025ae28`) is a mixed-tree artifact and no figure is taken from it.
- **Supersedes.** ~~narration-only 71 of 961~~ retracted — superseded by 62 of 961 on release `bc99dcc268ce`, same pins, same flags, same sample. ~~protocol diverging games 75~~ retracted — superseded by 66. Neither figure stands in a living document; both were printed by `engine/status.js`, which recomputes.
- **Owed to the next major.** `docs/ABRA-whitepaper.md` and `docs/SUMMARY.md` carry the MEDICHAM gate figures. Detail: `docs/_reports/2026-09-07-narration-batch-1.md`.

## [5.267.0] — 2026-09-07 — seven plant anchors were dead, three of them where `--reds` structurally cannot look, and the check that finds them now runs on every roster run
- **What changed.** `tests/roster.js` and `engine/quarantine.js`. No engine file was touched. (1) **SEVEN DEAD PLANT ANCHORS RE-AIMED, IN SIX RULES.** A red demonstration plants a break in the frozen release's bytes by exact string replace and must match exactly once; the three move rules `move/plain-attack`, `move/variable-power` and `move/recharge` share one anchor that matched **0** times after `dmgRangeOneHit`'s single-hit return grew `,type:mvT` (commit `1b5fd9f1`, 2026-09-07, the resist-berry type fix), `item/status-cure`'s matched **2** after `berryCureOnSet` landed the same morning carrying a byte-identical line, and `ability/trap-arrives-with-a-mega`, `ability/traps-and-somebody-tries-to-leave` and `ability/speed-on-item-loss` matched **0** after the trap refusal moved into `switchTrapVerdict` and the Unburden payment moved from `s*=` to `_mods.push(...)`. Nothing was weakened: `item/status-cure` is now aimed at `berryStatusCureNow`, the one function both cure schedules call, so the plant breaks **both** roads where the old anchor could only reach one. (2) **THE ANCHOR AUDIT IS UNCONDITIONAL.** The match check used to live inside the `--reds` loop; it is a string count that plays no game, so it is hoisted out and runs on every roster run, over every rule the stage assigned an entity to **including the COULD-NOT-STAGE ones**. A dead anchor is pushed into `reds` as an ordinary `ok: false` row, so it reaches the exit code and `quarantine.js`'s existing `badReds` term — **no new gate was added**. The `--reds` loop now consumes that result rather than re-applying the patch, so "does this anchor match once" has one implementation. Artifacts carry `plant_anchors: {release, checked, dead[], reds_ran}`. (3) `engine/quarantine.js` prints `--reds` in all three rerun commands, and its clause sentence now tells an empty `reds` from an unarmed one.
- **Measured.** All **87** rules with a break apply exactly once on release `1be57a100d59`. Red demonstrations **18 of 18 items, 29 of 29 abilities, 35 of 35 moves — 0 NOT CAUGHT** — `data/roster.{items,abilities,moves}.json`, release `1be57a100d59`, `--reds --write`. Roster counts unchanged in all three stages: **140 of 148 / 129 of 202 / 475 of 500 tested, 0 FIRED-AND-BOARDS-DIFFER and 0 DID-NOT-FIRE**. Anchors checked per stage 18 / 31 / 36, `dead: 0`.
- **THE THREE ABILITY RULES ARE THE PART `--reds` COULD NEVER HAVE FOUND.** Every member of all three is `COULD-NOT-STAGE`, and the reds loop is keyed on rules with at least one staged member — so the abilities stage read **29 of 29 CAUGHT** with three dead anchors inside it and would have gone on doing so. They are re-aimed and remain UNEXERCISED by construction: the claim restored is *the anchor names a live site*, not *the plant moves a board*, and the run prints those as two separate lines so they cannot be read as one.
- **Shown red before being trusted.** With the repair in, `move/recharge`'s live anchor was deliberately mutated (`_hits>1` to `_hits>99`) and the stage run **without** `--reds`: `0 of 1 apply exactly once / DEAD ANCHOR move/recharge matched 0 time(s), not 1 [6 row(s) in this stage, 6 of them staged]`, exit code **1**. Driven through the shipping `rosterStage`, an artifact carrying one `anchor_dead` row reads `ok=false` with `--reds` absent. The mutation was reverted and all 87 anchors re-audited.
- **The `--reds` hole — what was chosen.** The printed command arms it; an empty `reds` does **not** fail the clause. Failing on an unarmed `--reds` would redden the gate for a scheduling reason on an engine that is fine, and an over-firing gate is the one people learn to ignore. The free half — the anchor audit — does hold the gate, and it is the half that would have caught `,type:mvT` on the day. What was genuinely missing was distinguishability, and that is now a field: `plant_anchors.reds_ran`, reported as a denominator in the clause sentence.
- **Gate.** `node engine/status.js` reads **8 pass / 1 fail** — `deliberate roster / items` and `deliberate roster / moves` are green, and `whole-game differential / NARRATION` (**71 of 961**) is the only failing clause, untouched by this pass. Board-material stays **0 of 958**. Every quarantined figure stays withheld.
- **Red and named, not filed.** `engine/quarantine.js --selftest` is **232 passed, 1 failed**: *"the live gate carries exactly one reporting clause and it is the narration one"*, `got []`. It is **pre-existing and not caused by this pass** — `gates` for the narration clause is `!!(board && board.ok === true && board.pins)`, so narration started GATING rather than REPORTING the moment board-material reached 0 of 958 earlier today, leaving `reporting` empty. It belongs to the NARRATION clause, which this pass was explicitly scoped out of.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing. No roster count moved; the four NOT CAUGHT rows were an instrument failure, and the figures beside them were already correct.
- **Basis.** unchanged — PATCH. Repairing an instrument's self-test moves no published figure.
- **Owed to the next major.** `docs/MEASURE.md`. Full account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-07-red-plant-repair.md`.

## [5.267.0] — 2026-09-07 — the four stale gate clauses were re-run on current bytes, and running the roster's red demonstrations for the first time found four plants that no longer apply
- **What changed.** NO CODE. A re-measurement: the four gate clauses that were answering on release `c28ad0815782` while the tree is `1be57a100d59` were re-run on the current release. `data/engine-diff.json` (`tests/test-engine-diff.js --n 6000 --seed 20260804`), the three deliberate-roster stages (`tests/roster.js --stage {items,abilities,moves} --reds --write --release 1be57a100d59`) and `data/all-mechanics-fire.json` (`engine/all_mechanics_fire.js --kind all --write --release 1be57a100d59`). **`--reds` is not in the command the gate prints and is not optional**: all three roster artifacts on disk carried `reds: []`, so the safety net had never been asked on them.
- **Measured.** Differential **0 disagreements of 6000** at the midpoint, both corners and all fourteen interior roll indices — `data/engine-diff.json`, release `1be57a100d59`, seed `20260804`. Roster **140 of 148 items / 129 of 202 abilities / 475 of 500 moves**, all three at **0 FIRED-AND-BOARDS-DIFFER and 0 DID-NOT-FIRE** — `data/roster.{items,abilities,moves}.json`, release `1be57a100d59`. Mechanics **495 of 500 moves resolved, 104 abilities fired, 64 items fired, 5 divergences of which 1 declared and 4 below the reach shelf, leaving 0** — `data/all-mechanics-fire.json`, release `1be57a100d59`, arm `bottom-tie-first`, census pin `efdfde78903b` (830 rows). Every count is identical to the run on `c28ad0815782`: the three simulator fixes moved none of these four instruments.
- **AND FOUR RED DEMONSTRATIONS NO LONGER APPLY, WHICH IS THE FINDING.** `tests/roster.js` plants a break and asks whether the verdict flips; a plant whose anchor does not match exactly once is **unapplied**, and an unapplied plant reads exactly like a comparator that found nothing. Items: **17 of 18 CAUGHT**, `item/status-cure` NOT CAUGHT (anchor matched 2 times) — 5 green rows. Abilities: **29 of 29 CAUGHT**. Moves: **32 of 35 CAUGHT**, `move/plain-attack`, `move/variable-power` and `move/recharge` all NOT CAUGHT on one shared anchor that matched 0 times — **198 of that stage's 475 green rows, 41.7%**, counted off `data/roster.moves.json:results`. **NOT A REGRESSION FROM TONIGHT'S BATCH:** the anchor `return {min:roll(85),max:roll(100),eff};` is dead on `1be57a100d59` and on `c28ad0815782`, `09fde54aa1df`, `a31d271995e3` and `830350135192` alike; `git log -S 'eff,type:mvT}' -- engine/medicham2-browser.js` returns exactly one commit, `1b5fd9f1` at 2026-09-07 12:52:23 -0400, the resist-berry type fix that appended `type:mvT` to that return. 575 of 590 frozen releases still carry the old line. Nothing was adjusted to make a stage green; the anchors live in an instrument file this pass did not touch.
- **THE GATE MOVED 3 PASS / 6 FAIL TO 6 PASS / 3 FAIL AND THE QUARANTINE HAS NOT OPENED.** `engine/status.js` now reads `3 of 9 gate clauses fail`: `deliberate roster / items`, `deliberate roster / moves` — **both on the unapplied plants alone, not on any engine count** — and `whole-game differential / NARRATION`, red on its own merits at **71 of 961**, untouched by this pass. Every quarantined figure stays withheld.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing. The four artifacts' previous counts were WITHHELD by the gate, not published, and the re-measured counts equal them.
- **Basis.** unchanged — same instruments, same pins, same seed, same arm. One caveat stated rather than buried: the mechanics before/after spans a census regeneration (`dec15a5ed2e9` at 19:17:10Z to `efdfde78903b` at 20:56:57Z), so per `CLAUDE.md` that pair is not a before/after; the two runs agreeing on every count is evidence and not a controlled comparison. `data/all-mechanics-fire.json` records no census digest of its own — the pin is only printed to the run log.
- **Owed to the next major.** `docs/MEASURE.md` and the quarantine narrative in the white paper, the deck, `docs/SUMMARY.md` and `docs/MODELS.md` — the gate is now two roster plants and one narration clause away from a different story. Full account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-07-stale-clause-rerun.md`.

## [5.267.0] — 2026-09-07 — the last three games that parted a board are closed, and the board-material clause reads zero
- **What changed.** `engine/medicham2-browser.js`, three fixes measured one at a time, each on its own frozen release. (1) **LAST RESORT'S PRECONDITION IS PER STAY ON THE FIELD.** `moveSlot.used` is cleared by `switchIn` (`sim/battle-actions.ts:136-140`) and PP is not, so "this slot has been clicked" and "this slot is below its maximum" are different statements — which this engine's own comment asserted they were not. `_usedEntry` is written inside `ppDeduct` (the mirror of `deductPP` setting `ppData.used` above its own PP check) and cleared in `bringIn`. (2) **THE PER-ARRIVAL RE-PRICE SERVES A NON-FLAT VOLLEY.** `dmgRange` gains `onlyHitNo`, which runs its existing per-hit loop for one value of `h`, so a resist berry spent by arrival 1 stops halving arrival 2 on a Parental Bond click; `hits` is deliberately left alone on that road because `hitPlanOf` reads it as a rolled count. (3) **A SPLIT `smartTarget` VOLLEY IS RESOLVED ONE BODY AT A TIME.** The driver walks `_stepDamage` .. `_stepAfterHitField` row-major for a split volley, which is `hitStepMoveHitLoop`'s own shape (`data/mods/champions/scripts.ts:467-518`); `_stepUpdate` is left step-major on purpose. No new die is drawn by any of the three. Probes `tests/probe_lastresort_entry.js`, `tests/probe_bond_arrival_reprice.js`, `tests/probe_smarttarget_row_order.js`; knobs `MEDI_LASTRESORT_BATTLE_USED`, `MEDI_ARRIVAL_REPRICE_FLAT_ONLY`, `MEDI_SMARTTARGET_STEP_MAJOR`.
- **Measured.** Board-material **3 of 958 → 0 of 958**, protocol (diverging games) **76 → 75**, `diverged_among_usable` **73 → 72** — `data/game-differential.json`, release `1be57a100d59`, `--games 1200` (961 played, 958 readable), `--turns 20`, arm `middle`, `empirical-click/v1`, census pin `9446a684709d`, `--team-store data/team-pool-frozen`. Per-fix releases `a31d271995e3` (2 of 958), `09fde54aa1df` (1 of 958), `1be57a100d59` (0 of 958). Census **830/830 live, 0 missing** — `data/mechanics-census.json`, regenerated on the fixed bytes, unchanged. Predictions written before each run in `data/verification/_prediction-2026-09-07-batchN-{lastresort,bondreprice,smarttarget}.json`; all three hit on every clause, and neither of the two risks named in advance materialised — no game arrived, none transferred.
- **THE QUARANTINE HAS NOT OPENED AND NO DOWNSTREAM FIGURE IS RELEASED HERE.** `engine/status.js` reads `PASS whole-game differential / BOARD-MATERIAL — 0 of 958` and **6 of 9 clauses still fail**: `NARRATION` is red on its own merits at 71 of 961 (the separate gate Will called for on 2026-08-22 — a game that stops parting a board moves bucket rather than leaving the run), and four clauses are STALE rather than broken, because `data/engine-diff.json`, the three roster stages and `data/all-mechanics-fire.json` all ran on `c28ad0815782` while the tree is `1be57a100d59`. Those four are owed a re-run. Every quarantined figure stays withheld.
- **Owed and loud.** The non-flat re-price **disarms itself on 39 of the 180 non-flat clicks** in this pool at its own arrival-0 invariant (`arrivalRepriceDriftsAtArrivalZero`, printed by the run: `offered 490, ran 850, MOVED 14, drifted 39`; baseline read `offered 349, drifted 0`, and 141 + 39 = 180). A drifted click is disarmed, not mispriced — it behaves exactly as it did before this batch. Separately, a multi-arrival drain is paid once at the foot of the volley instead of once per arrival (`showdown 2 drain lines, medicham 1`, same final HP) — narration, printed on every run of `tests/probe_bond_arrival_reprice.js`.
- **Supersedes.** Board-material ~~3 of 958~~ retracted and protocol ~~76~~ retracted, both on release `c28ad0815782`.
- **Basis.** unchanged — same instrument (`driver code f360a8681749` over 11 files, reported unchanged across every run), same pins, same pinned pool, same census pin, same `--games 1200`. A reader can be told "3 became 0"; the question has not moved.
- **Owed to the next major.** The white paper, the deck, `docs/SUMMARY.md` and `docs/MODELS.md` — and the quarantine narrative in each of them, which is now one clause plus four stale artifacts away from a different story rather than five open engine defects.

## [5.267.0] — 2026-09-07 — the board bar was quoted over a denominator the protocol rate beside it never used, and two engine fixes under the corrected ruler
- **What changed — ONE INSTRUMENT CORRECTION AND TWO ENGINE FIXES, MEASURED SEPARATELY.** (1) **THE RULER.** `engine/game_differential.js`: `STATE_SUMMARY` walked `results` unfiltered while `mid_void`, printed two lines above it, published its protocol rate over `usable_games` — so the board bar charged the engine for three `low-identity` games the instrument itself declares unreadable. The state population is now the usable set; the exclusion is PUBLISHED (`state.games_void_excluded`, `state.games_before_void_exclusion`), the void games are NAMED (`mid_void.void_game_tags`), and `--state-count-void` reproduces the old population exactly. (2) `engine/medicham2-browser.js`: a volley is priced ONCE before any arrival lands, so a stat change between two arrivals is invisible to the later one — the price step now stores each arrival's roll INDEX and the apply loop re-prices one arrival against the current board, drawing NO new die. Flat volleys only; Triple Axel and Parental Bond are refused and counted. (3) `engine/medicham2-browser.js`: the ABILITY's fractional-priority die is drawn before the ITEM's, which is `onFractionalPriorityPriority` -1 before -2. Probes `tests/probe_state_void_exclusion.js`, `tests/probe_arrival_reprice.js`, `tests/probe_fracpri_die_order.js`; knobs `--state-count-void`, `MEDI_ARRIVAL_PRICE_ONCE`, `MEDI_FRACPRI_ITEM_DIE_FIRST`.
- **Measured.** Board-material **8 of 961 → 3 of 958**, protocol **77 → 76**, narration **68 → 69** — `data/game-differential.json`, release `c28ad0815782`, `--games 1200` (961 played), `--turns 20`, arm `middle`, `empirical-click/v1`, census pin `9446a684709d`, `--team-store data/team-pool-frozen`. Census 830/830/0 (`data/mechanics-census.json`); roster 140/129/475 matched with 0 DIFFER and 0 DID-NOT-FIRE (`data/roster.{items,abilities,moves}.json`); `data/engine-diff.json` 0 of 6000 and `data/all-mechanics-fire.json`, both re-published on `c28ad0815782`.
- **THREE OF THE FIVE GAMES ARE THE RULER AND TWO ARE THE ENGINE, AND THE ATTRIBUTION IS MEASURED RATHER THAN ASSERTED.** Four runs, one variable each: OLD ruler on `3f9830acc467` **8 of 961**; OLD ruler on `830350135192` **7 of 961**; NEW ruler on `3f9830acc467` **5 of 958**; NEW ruler on `c28ad0815782` **3 of 958** — the two old-ruler arms in `data/verification/batchM/gd-oldruler-3f9830acc467.json` and `data/verification/batchM/gd-oldruler-830350135192.json`. `engine/arms_comparable.js` answers NOT COMPARABLE on the raw pair because the instrument moved, correctly, and that table is the answer to it.
- **Supersedes.** Board-material ~~8 of 961~~ and protocol ~~77~~ on release `3f9830acc467`, narration ~~68~~.
- **Basis.** unchanged — and that is a measured claim, not a convenience. The denominator moved, so it was BACK-CAST: the current driver reproduces the pre-batch **8 of 961** exactly under `--state-count-void`, and the new engine reads **7 of 961** on that same old ruler. A reader can be told "8 became 3, of which 3 games were the ruler and 2 the engine", which is a series that links rather than a question nobody asks any more. The pins, the pool and the census pin are unchanged throughout.
- **Owed to the next major.** The white paper, the deck, `docs/SUMMARY.md` and `docs/MODELS.md` — the DENOMINATOR as much as the figures: board-material is now quoted over the games the instrument can read, which is what the protocol rate beside it has always used.

## [5.267.0] — 2026-09-06 — seventeen unstamped arms re-taken under the instrument stamp, and the state ladder is withdrawn rather than re-run
- **What changed.** No engine or instrument code. **MEASURE re-ran seventeen `steering` artifacts that carried no `driver_code`**, each at the exact pins of the artifact it re-measures — `--games 1200` (961 played), `--turns 12`, `--end-state`, `--arm middle`, `--steering empirical`, `--census data/verification/census-pin-9446a684709d.json`, `--team-store data/team-pool-frozen`, `--release <that artifact's own id>`. Results in `data/verification/restamp/*.961.json`; the seventeen originals were **not overwritten and not deleted**. `data/state-ladder.json`'s ladder figures were **deleted from six living documents** (white paper, deck, technical docs, `GAME-DIFFERENTIAL-DESIGN`, `MODELS`, `SUMMARY`).
- **Measured.** On **identical engine bytes and an identical sample** — same release, same 961 games, same pool `0d103fb9fa87`/1,968 picked, same 643-row census, verified per arm 17 of 17 — **board-material rose by a mean of +22.2 (range +15 to +24)** and **protocol first-divergence by +32.6 (+20 to +38)**. Example: `data/verification/fix-batch-7.json` release `316669459d67` reads board-material 37 of 961 / protocol 122; `data/verification/restamp/fix-batch-7.961.json`, same release, reads **53 / 143**. The whole difference is the ruler. `engine/arms_comparable.js`: **NEW vs NEW `COMPARABLE` 16 of 16**, driver CHECKED both sides; **OLD vs NEW `NOT COMPARABLE` 17 of 17**, the only proven-different reason in all seventeen being `mode` (pin digest `bcb38e47d94f` → `de38d17e15a2`, the `sdrop` address category) plus `UNKNOWN — only the after-arm records driver_code` 17 of 17. Nothing about the sample is refused anywhere. Fifteen of sixteen adjacent steps keep their sign; the exception is `M5M7M8 → M6-sidesel`, a derived +3 regression that reads 0 on today's instrument.
- **Basis.** unchanged — and the judgement is stated so it can be argued with. No published figure is replaced: each living document names the artifact it quotes, that artifact still contains that number, and it remains a true statement about the ruler that took it. What is added is a second, stamped series that must **never** be tabled against the first — which `arms_comparable` refuses seventeen times rather than leaving to a reader.
- **Supersedes.** The turn-1 and whole-game board-agreement figures drawn from `data/state-ladder.json` are **retracted and not replaced**; they have been DELETED from the six living documents named above, not captioned. Three measured reasons: the artifact carries no `steering.driver_code` so every pair drawn from it reads UNKNOWN and no work today can repair that; its own `determinism.verdict` is red and no document quoting it ever mentioned that; and its pool was drawn live from `data/games.bo3.jsonl` at `ff6529f6a6d7`, a month of hourly appends ago, so the sample cannot be re-taken. A re-run would answer a different question, which is worse than a withdrawal. **Nothing was back-stamped, and no tool that could was built or found** — re-checked independently tonight.
- **Owed to the next major.** `docs/ENGINE.md` still carries the retracted state-ladder figures at `:35070` and `:35089` — it is ENGINE's ledger and carries no version header, so it is outside the living-document set; **filed, not edited.** `data/wire-ladder.json` was NOT re-run (already UNSAFE and WITHHELD; its August pool cannot be recovered either) and `data/protocol-events.json` is still in no digest set at all. A suspected false alarm in `engine/wire_ladder.js`'s determinism check is filed and unfixed — the per-arm artifacts needed to settle it were not kept. Full account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-06-comparability-restore.md`.

## [5.267.0] — 2026-09-06 — the counter tables get the reader they always had, and the PDF rule grows from the ledger to the working document

- **What changed — the red test.** `tests/test-artifact-keys.js` had been red since before this
  session on `UNDECLARED: million-run-150k.json:engine_counters, million-run.json:engine_counters`.
  **The artifact side was right and the 2026-09-04 premise was wrong.** *Nothing reads them* held for
  PROGRAMMATIC readers and was read as true of all readers. The dead-counters audit derives its
  PROVEN BY RUN / UNMEASURED split — 134 fields and 363 — from which keys are present in those two
  artifacts and nowhere else, and `tests/test-counter-init.js` exists BECAUSE both carry
  `retaliateWhenLowered: null`, the only non-finite value in either. Both tables are now declared
  against the one function that indexes them, `instrumentChecks(rows, counters)` at
  `engine/million_run.js:1269`, two keys by hard-coded literal — with the limit stated in the entry:
  nothing reads the FILE’s copy by key. `engine/million_run.js` is UNCHANGED. The other remedy could
  not clear the red and would have destroyed that evidence: the gate judges the two files ON DISK,
  both were generated 2026-08-11 and both carry `GENERATED — do not hand-edit`, and regenerating them
  means playing games through MEDICHAM. ROADMAP #539 is closed with the refutation written beside the
  original text rather than over it; its `engine_counters_zero` half stays open. The fitted weights
  were not touched.
- **What changed — the PDF rule.** `build/build_pdfs.js` now excludes WORKING DOCUMENTS rather than
  only division ledgers. Clause 1 stays derived from `.claude/agents/*.md`; clause 2 is a DECLARED
  RESIDUAL — `docs/ROADMAP.md` and `docs/RUNNING-NOTES.md` — with a reason each, and the clause is
  printed beside every excluded name so a declared entry can never read as a derived one. **The shared
  property could not be derived, and five candidates were measured over all 78 `docs/*.md` before
  saying so:** named-by-a-program matched 56 of them; named-in-code-with-comments-stripped still 45,
  and it catches DEFENSE, METHODOLOGY and ORIENTATION, which have published PDF links; only 24 carry
  a version header, so the currency clause would drop 54 PDFs; body shape does not separate at all
  (ROADMAP 44.3% table rows, RUNNING-NOTES 0.0%, SUMMARY 22.4%, ROLE-ATLAS 98.8%); and a git-history
  rule cannot classify a document created this week, which is the one that most needed classifying.
  **The residual is CHECKED, not trusted.** The build THROWS on an entry naming a document that is not
  there, on an entry that has become a ledger, and — the clause that protects the saving — on ANY
  excluded document whose PDF is still tracked. `docs/ROADMAP.pdf` is untracked (`git rm --cached`,
  file left on disk) and both names are in `docs/.gitignore`.
- **Measured.** NO MODEL FIGURE MOVED. `node tests/test-artifact-keys.js` reads **5 passed / 1 failed
  / exit 1 before** and **6 passed / 0 failed / exit 0 after**, with the detector untouched: 53 tables
  found, 37 flat-lowercase and structurally immune, **16 that can be missed before and after**, all 16
  now declared. Shown RED on a deliberate break first — removing one declaration reprints the
  UNDECLARED clause by name, and pointing another at a missing file fails the dangling-accessor clause
  (4 passed / 2 failed). The repository figures are derived from git rather than from an artifact:
  `docs/ROADMAP.pdf` is **7,229,637 B** in the tree across **eight** tracked versions holding **16.42
  MB raw / 5.19 MB packed**, and the version at the most recent bump `d7ed4b75` packs to **4,092,583 B
  — 4.09 MB of pack per rebuild**. `docs/RUNNING-NOTES.pdf` has never been built; priced once to a
  scratch path it is **241,109 B** today at 190 lines, about 0.14 MB packed at ROADMAP’s 57% ratio.
  **Per-bump saving ≈ 4.2 MB of pack**, on top of the 16.6 MB the ledger clause already removed.
  **UNTRACKING RECOVERS ZERO BYTES OF EXISTING HISTORY** — the blobs stay in the pack, only a history
  rewrite removes them, and the saving is entirely future. `node build/build_pdfs.js --check` **exited
  1 before** on exactly two items, a stale `ROADMAP.pdf` and a missing `RUNNING-NOTES.pdf`, and **exits
  0 after** — not because anything was rebuilt, but because those two items were the two documents the
  rule now excludes. Each of the three new refusals was shown red on a deliberate break.
- **Basis.** unchanged. No published figure moved, and nothing in either job touches a model, a
  simulator or a fitted weight.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing. No figure changed value; the 2026-09-04 DEAD TABLE disposition was a
  judgement, not a figure, and it is recorded as superseded rather than deleted.
- **Owed to the next major.** `docs/ABRA-technical-docs.md` and `docs/SUMMARY.md` describe the
  publishing procedure and must record that a working document — ledger, register or notes log —
  carries no PDF. `docs/ARTIFACT-ACCESS-RULES.md` must record that the engine counter delta is
  declared against an in-generator reader.

## [5.267.0] — 2026-09-06 — a major release is defined, and the counter that was to bound the deferral had never read a row

- **What changed.** `CLAUDE.md` gained *What counts as a major*: ABRA's declared public API is the
  FIGURES it publishes (SemVer 2.0.0 clause 1 permits an API existing "strictly in documentation"), so
  PATCH moves no published figure, MINOR moves one under an unchanged basis, and MAJOR moves the basis
  — the ESS *Guidelines on Revision Policy* (Eurostat 2013, KS-RA-13-016) Items 2.0/3.0/3.2/3.4
  routine-versus-major revision distinction, whose back-casting and re-documentation obligations are
  exactly the living-document fold-in. The judgement is DECLARED in one word per row (`**Basis.**`);
  `engine/docs_scan.js` gained `majorPolicy()` and `tests/test-docs-current.js` gained clause 5d,
  which refuses a basis change released as anything but `X.0.0`, an `X.0.0` naming no basis change,
  and a PATCH bump whose row supersedes a figure.
- **AND A DEFECT WAS FOUND IN THE INSTRUMENT THE WHOLE RULE RESTS ON.** `notesEntries()` anchors its
  row pattern with `$`; `core.autocrlf` is `true` here, so the LF blob of this page checks out CRLF,
  and a CR is a JavaScript line terminator that `$` cannot reach past. The backlog therefore reported
  **`0 of 100` — "nothing owed"** against a page holding **four** rows, while
  `tests/test-docs-current.js` passed **30 of 30**. A counter stuck at zero can never reach
  `OWED_CAP`, so the cap could never have fired and the deferral was unbounded. Fixed at the read
  (`stripCR`), not in the pattern; `.gitattributes` already carries a block headed *"A LINE ENDING
  BLANKED THE GATE TWICE IN THREE DAYS"* and this is the third occurrence.
- **Measured.** Backlog **0 → 4 of 100** owed, `node engine/docs_scan.js --owed`, read from
  `docs/RUNNING-NOTES.md`, `CHANGELOG.md` and the living-document headers; documents last folded at
  5.266.0, last major 5.0.0 (2026-08-10). Blast radius of the read fix over the whole document
  surface: living documents **25 → 25**, citation mismatches **78 → 78**, notes entries **0 → 4** —
  only the broken derivation moved. **23 of the live documents are CRLF on disk** and 0 now leak a CR
  to a parser. The gate went **30 passed / 0 failed → 33 passed / 0 failed**; every ratchet in
  `data/docs-currency-baseline.json` is unmoved and the file was not rewritten.
- **Basis.** unchanged. No figure this project publishes changed its meaning or its value; a counter
  that had never counted began counting.
- **Supersedes.** ~~0 of 100 notes entries owed to the next major~~ retracted — it was printed by
  `node engine/docs_scan.js --owed` and by `node engine/open_work.js` on 2026-09-06 and stood in no
  living document, so it is withdrawn here rather than deleted from one.
- **Owed to the next major.** `docs/ABRA-technical-docs.md` and `docs/SUMMARY.md` describe the
  documentation and release procedure; both must record the major/minor/patch definition and that the
  cap owes a document pass rather than a major.
